# childhood.90 Conscientious: the scene
# The child meets one of three scenes picked by the court around them, with
# a courtier saved as the other party. What the child does decides whether
# they turn conscientious.
# Scope: ROOT is the child. Fired from childhood.1.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.90
	picture = GFX_evt_child_talking

	# Version 1
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood90a # Messy room, sort the books or go play?
		picture = GFX_evt_ritual_scroll
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_1 }
	}
	# Version 2
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood90b # child misbehaves, tell an adult or keep secret?
		picture = GFX_evt_council
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_2 }
	}
	# Version 3
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood90c # lost clothing outside, go look for it or go home?
		picture = GFX_evt_child_play
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_3 }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = ze_child_conscientious_events }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = { #Find viable courtier or if none, only make third version available
		set_character_flag = ze_child_conscientious_events
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					educator = {
						NOT = {
							any_courtier = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOR = {
									trait = incapable
									has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
									has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
								}
								NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
							}
						}
					}
					factor = 0
				}
				set_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_1
				educator = {
					random_courtier = {
						limit = {
							is_adult = yes
							NOR = {
								trait = incapable
								has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
								has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
							}
							NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
						}
						save_event_target_as = conscientious_events_char_1
						if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
							limit = { ROOT = { ai = yes } ai = yes }
							ROOT = {
								clr_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_1
								add_trait = conscientious
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					educator = {
						NOT = {
							any_courtier = {
								is_adult = no
								NOR = {
									trait = conscientious
									is_rival = ROOT
									is_friend = ROOT
									trait = imbecile
									trait = incapable
									has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
									has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
								}
								NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
							}
						}
					}
					factor = 0
				}
				set_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_2
				educator = {
					random_courtier = {
						limit = {
							is_adult = no
							NOR = {
								trait = conscientious
								is_rival = ROOT
								is_friend = ROOT
								trait = imbecile
								trait = incapable
								has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
								has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
							}
							NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
						}
						save_event_target_as = conscientious_events_char_2
						if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
							limit = { ROOT = { ai = yes } ai = yes }
							ROOT = {
								clr_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_2
								add_trait = conscientious
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_3
				if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
					limit = { ai = yes }
					clr_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_3
					add_trait = conscientious
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Version 1
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood90 # Sort the books
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_1 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		prestige = 10
		set_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_1_sorted_books
		event_target:conscientious_events_char_1 = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.92 } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCON }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood90 # Go out and play
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_1 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		health = 1
		set_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_1_go_play
		event_target:conscientious_events_char_1 = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.92 } }
		}
	}

	# Version 2
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCchildhood90 # Child misbehaving, you tell on him/her
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_2 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		set_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_2_told_on
		event_target:conscientious_events_char_2 = {
			add_rival = ROOT
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.92 } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTCON }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDchildhood90 # Keep the secret
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_2 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		set_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_2_kept_quiet
		event_target:conscientious_events_char_2 = {
			add_friend = ROOT
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.92 } }
		}
	}

	# Version 3
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEchildhood90 # Go look for missing clothing
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_3 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		add_trait = conscientious
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTEchildhood90_tooltip1 }
				hidden_tooltip = {
					set_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_3_pneumonia
					character_event = { id = childhood.94 }
				}
			}
			50 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTEchildhood90_tooltip2 }
				hidden_tooltip = {
					set_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_3_found_clothes
					character_event = { id = childhood.94 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFchildhood90 # Go back home
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_3 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		change_diplomacy = 1
		prestige = 20
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.97 } } # Clear all flags
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFchildhood50 # ai option, nothing to see here
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_1
				has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_2
				has_character_flag = child_conscientious_version_3
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

		}

		tooltip = { add_friend = FROM }
		FROM = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.97 } } # Clear all flags

# childhood.100 Fussy: the scene
# The child meets one of three scenes picked by the court around them, with
# a courtier saved as the other party. What the child does decides whether
# they turn fussy.
# Scope: ROOT is the child. Fired from childhood.1.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.100
	picture = GFX_evt_child_talking

	# Version 1
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood100a # About to go to bed, but missing toy
		picture = GFX_evt_cathedral
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_fussy_version_1 }
	}
	# Version 2
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood100b # Food is served, but is very hot
		picture = GFX_evt_feast
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_fussy_version_2 }
	}
	# Version 3
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood100c # Invited to eat cake but it's raining outside
		picture = GFX_evt_quarrel
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_fussy_version_3 }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = ze_child_fussy_events }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = { #Find viable courtier or if none, only make third version available
		set_character_flag = ze_child_fussy_events
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					educator = {
						OR = {
							trait = blinded
							trait = imbecile
							trait = incapable
							has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
							has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
						}
					}
					factor = 0
				}
				set_character_flag = child_fussy_version_1
				educator = {
					save_event_target_as = fussy_events_char_1
					if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
						limit = { ROOT = { ai = yes } ai = yes }
						ROOT = { clr_character_flag = child_fussy_version_1 add_trait = fussy }
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					educator = {
						NOT = {
							any_courtier = {
								NOR = {
									trait = blinded
									trait = imbecile
									trait = incapable
									has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
									has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
								}
								NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
							}
						}
					}
					factor = 0
				}
				set_character_flag = child_fussy_version_2
				educator = {
					random_courtier = {
						limit = {
							NOR = {
								trait = blinded
								trait = imbecile
								trait = incapable
								has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
								has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
							}
							NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
						}
						save_event_target_as = fussy_events_char_2
						if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
							limit = { ROOT = { ai = yes } ai = yes }
							ROOT = { clr_character_flag = child_fussy_version_2 add_trait = fussy }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = child_fussy_version_3
				if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
					limit = { ai = yes }
					clr_character_flag = child_fussy_version_3
					add_trait = fussy
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Version 1
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood100 # wont go to bed without toy
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_fussy_version_1 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		set_character_flag = child_fussy_version_1_refused
		event_target:fussy_events_char_1 = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.102 } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTFUS }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood100 # toy is not that important
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_fussy_version_1 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		prestige = 40
		set_character_flag = child_fussy_version_1_compliant
		event_target:fussy_events_char_1 = {
			tooltip = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_proud years = 2 } }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.102 } }
		}
	}

	# Version 2
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCchildhood100 # Food is too hot
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_fussy_version_2 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		set_character_flag = child_fussy_version_2_too_hot
		event_target:fussy_events_char_2 = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.102 } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTFUS }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDchildhood100 # eat food and complement cooking
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_fussy_version_2 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		tooltip = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_happy years = 2 } }
		set_character_flag = child_fussy_version_2_complemented_food
		event_target:fussy_events_char_2 = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.102 } }
		}
	}

	# Version 3
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEchildhood100 # Go out in the rain
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_fussy_version_3 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		prestige = 20
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTEchildhood100_tooltip1 }
				hidden_tooltip = {
					set_character_flag = child_fussy_version_3_made_it
					character_event = { id = childhood.104 }
				}
			}
			50 = {

				hidden_tooltip = {
					set_character_flag = child_fussy_version_3_became_ill
					character_event = { id = childhood.104 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {

		set_character_flag = child_fussy_version_3_waited
		add_trait = fussy
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.107 } } #Clear all flags
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFchildhood50 # ai option, nothing to see here
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_character_flag = child_fussy_version_1
				has_character_flag = child_fussy_version_2
				has_character_flag = child_fussy_version_3
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

		clr_character_flag = child_fussy_version_3_became_ill

# childhood.110 Curious: the scene
# The child meets one of three scenes picked by the court around them, with
# a courtier saved as the other party. What the child does decides whether
# they turn curious.
# Scope: ROOT is the child. Fired from childhood.1.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.110
	picture = GFX_evt_child_talking

	# Version 1
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood110a # Follow adult when told to stay at home?
		picture = GFX_evt_guardian
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_1 }
	}
	# Version 2
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood110b # Open sealed letter meant for adult?
		picture = GFX_evt_recieve_letter
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_2 }
	}
	# Version 3
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood110c # Sounds coming from the bushes, investigate?
		picture = GFX_evt_child_play
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_3 }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = ze_child_curious_events }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = { #Find viable courtier or if none, only make third version available
		set_character_flag = ze_child_curious_events
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					educator = {
						OR = {
							NOT = { trait = charitable }
							trait = incapable
							has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
							has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
							is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					factor = 0
				}
				set_character_flag = child_curious_version_1
				educator = {
					save_event_target_as = curious_events_char_1
					if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
						limit = { ROOT = { ai = yes } ai = yes }
						ROOT = { clr_character_flag = child_curious_version_1 add_trait = curious }
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					educator = {
						OR = {
							trait = imbecile
							trait = incapable
							has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
							has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
							is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					factor = 0
				}
				set_character_flag = child_curious_version_2
				educator = {
					save_event_target_as = curious_events_char_2
					if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
						limit = { ROOT = { ai = yes } ai = yes }
						ROOT = { clr_character_flag = child_curious_version_2 add_trait = curious }
					}
				}
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = child_curious_version_3
				if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
					limit = { ai = yes }
					clr_character_flag = child_curious_version_3
					add_trait = curious
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Version 1
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood110 # Follow our educator
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_1 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		add_trait = curious
		set_character_flag = child_curious_version_1_followed
		event_target:curious_events_char_1 = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.112 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood110 # Stay at home like a good boy/girl
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_1 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		piety = 50
		set_character_flag = child_curious_version_1_stayed_home
		event_target:curious_events_char_1 = {
			tooltip = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_satisfied years = 2 } }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.112 } }
		}
	}

	# Version 2
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCchildhood110 # Put the letter on a table for our educator
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_2 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		piety = 50
		tooltip = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_glad years = 2 } }
		set_character_flag = child_curious_version_2_left_letter
		event_target:curious_events_char_2 = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.112 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDchildhood110 # Open the letter
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_2 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		add_trait = curious
		set_character_flag = child_curious_version_2_read_letter
		event_target:curious_events_char_2 = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.112 } }
		}
	}

	# Version 3
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEchildhood110 # Investigate strange noise in bushes
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_3 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		add_trait = curious
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTEchildhood110_tooltip1 }
				hidden_tooltip = {
					set_character_flag = child_curious_version_3_bit
					character_event = { id = childhood.114 }
				}
			}
			50 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTEchildhood110_tooltip2 }
				hidden_tooltip = {
					set_character_flag = child_curious_version_3_found_pet
					character_event = { id = childhood.114 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFchildhood110 # Go tell someone
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_3 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.117 } } #Clear all flags
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFchildhood50 # ai option, nothing to see here
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_character_flag = child_curious_version_1
				has_character_flag = child_curious_version_2
				has_character_flag = child_curious_version_3
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

# childhood.112 Curious: the courtier reacts
# The courtier answers what the child did.
# Scope: ROOT is the courtier, FROM the child.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.112
	picture = GFX_evt_child_talking

	# Version 1
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood112a # Child followed me to market
		picture = GFX_evt_guardian
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_1_followed } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood112b # Child waited patiently at home
		picture = GFX_evt_child_reading
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_1_stayed_home } }
	}
	# Version 2
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood112c # Child opened my private letter
		picture = GFX_evt_recieve_letter
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_2_read_letter } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood112d # Child left my letter untouched
		picture = GFX_evt_recieve_letter
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_2_left_letter } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
	}

	# Version 1
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood112 # Grab child by ear and correct them
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_1_followed } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 70 }
		if = { limit = { religion = shallyan } piety = -15 }
		prestige = 30
		FROM = {
			set_character_flag = child_curious_version_1_scolded
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.114 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood112 # Laugh it off
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_1_followed } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
		prestige = -40
		FROM = {
			tooltip = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_happy_with_guardian years = 2 } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				set_character_flag = child_curious_version_1_forgiven
				character_event = { id = childhood.114 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCchildhood112 # Child stayed at home, you are glad
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_1_stayed_home } }
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_satisfied years = 2 }
		FROM = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.117 } } #Clear all flags
		}
	}

	# Version 2
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDchildhood112 # Punish child for opening your letter
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_2_read_letter } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
		if = { limit = { religion = shallyan } piety = -15 }
		FROM = {
			set_character_flag = child_curious_version_2_punished
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.114 } }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEchildhood112 # Tell child about privacy
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_2_read_letter } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 60 }
		FROM = {
			tooltip = { prestige = -30 }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				set_character_flag = child_curious_version_2_warned
				character_event = { id = childhood.114 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFchildhood112 # You're glad child didn't open the letter
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_curious_version_2_left_letter } }
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_glad years = 2 }
		FROM = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.117 } } #Clear all flags
		}
	}
}

# childhood.120 Idolizer: the scene
# The child meets one of three scenes picked by the court around them, with
# a courtier saved as the other party. What the child does decides whether
# they turn idolizer.
# Scope: ROOT is the child. Fired from childhood.1.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.120
	picture = GFX_evt_child_talking

	# Version 1
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood120a # Amazed by courtier
		picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1 }
	}
	# Version 2
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood120b # Adore other child, love?
		picture = GFX_evt_child_talking
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2 }
	}
	# Version 3
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood120c # All consuming devotion to god
		picture = GFX_evt_bishop
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_3 }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = ze_child_idolizer_events }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = { #Find viable courtier or if none, only make third version available
		set_character_flag = ze_child_idolizer_events
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						educator = {
							NOT = {
								any_courtier = {
									is_adult = yes
									NOR = {
										trait = incapable
										trait = wroth
										trait = cruel
										trait = cynical
										has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
										has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
									}
									NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
								}
							}
						}
						factor = 0
					}
					set_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1
					educator = {
						random_courtier = {
							limit = {
								is_adult = yes
								NOR = {
									trait = incapable
									trait = wroth
									trait = cruel
									trait = cynical
									has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
									has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
								}
								NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
							}
							save_event_target_as = idolizer_events_char_1
							if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
								limit = { ROOT = { ai = yes } ai = yes }
								ROOT = {
									clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1
									add_trait = idolizer
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				10 = {
					modifier = {
						educator = {
							NOT = {
								any_courtier = {
									is_adult = no
									NOR = {
										trait = imbecile
										trait = incapable
										has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
										has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
									}
									NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
								}
							}
						}
						factor = 0
					}
					set_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2
					educator = {
						random_courtier = {
							limit = {
								is_adult = no
								NOR = {
									trait = imbecile
									trait = incapable
									has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
									has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
								}
								NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
							}
							save_event_target_as = idolizer_events_char_2
							if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
								limit = { ROOT = { ai = yes } ai = yes }
								ROOT = {
									clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2
									add_trait = idolizer
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				10 = {
					set_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_3
					if = { # If we don't involve a player, skip these events
						limit = { ai = yes }
						clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_3
						add_trait = idolizer
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	# Version 1
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood120 # write a song to specific courtier
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		set_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_wrote_song
		event_target:idolizer_events_char_1 = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.122 } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTIDO }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood120 # tell all courtiers how nice it is here
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		set_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_grateful
		event_target:idolizer_events_char_1 = {
			any_courtier = { opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_happy years = 3 } }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.122 } }
		}
	}

	# Version 2
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCchildhood120 # Everything event_target does is great
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		set_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_adore
		event_target:idolizer_events_char_2 = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.122 } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTIDO }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDchildhood120 # We should stay friends forever
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		set_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_friends
		event_target:idolizer_events_char_2 = {
			add_friend = ROOT
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.122 } }
		}
	}

	# Version 3
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEchildhood120 # Dedicate myself to the faith
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_3 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		piety = 30
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.124 } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTIDO }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFchildhood120 # steal money from church
		trigger = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_3 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		change_intrigue = 1
		wealth = 40
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.127 } } #Clear all flags
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFchildhood50 # ai option, nothing to see here
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1
				has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2
				has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_3
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

# childhood.122 Idolizer: the courtier reacts
# The courtier answers what the child did.
# Scope: ROOT is the courtier, FROM the child.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.122
	picture = GFX_evt_child_talking

	# Version 1
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood122a # Child wrote me a song
		picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_wrote_song } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood122b # Child is grateful to everyone
		picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_grateful } }
	}
	# Version 2
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood122c # Other child adores me
		picture = GFX_evt_child_talking
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_adore } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood122d # Other child wants to stay friends
		picture = GFX_evt_child_talking
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_friends } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette }
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
	}

	# Version 1
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood122 # Tell child they are sweet
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_wrote_song } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		prestige = 30
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_happy years = 5 }
		FROM = {
			set_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_brag
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.124 } }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTIDO_FROM }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood122 # I'm just doing my duties
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_wrote_song } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		prestige = 20
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = humble } } add_trait = humble }
		FROM = {
			hidden_tooltip = {
				set_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_humble
				character_event = { id = childhood.124 }
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTIDO_FROM }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCchildhood122 # Child shows gratitude to everyone
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_grateful } }
		tooltip = { any_courtier = { opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_happy years = 2 } } }
		FROM = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.127 } } #Clear all flags
		}
	}

	# Version 2
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDchildhood122 # I like being around you too
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_adore } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		FROM = {
			set_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_agree
			character_event = { id = childhood.124 }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTIDO_FROM }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEchildhood122 # I don't feel the same way
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_adore } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		change_diplomacy = 1
		FROM = {
			tooltip = { prestige = -30 }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				set_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_disagree
				character_event = { id = childhood.124 }
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFchildhood122 # What a good friend
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_friend } }
		tooltip = { add_friend = FROM }
		FROM = {
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.127 } } #Clear all flags
		}
	}
}

# childhood.127 Idolizer: flags cleared
# Clears the chain's version flags.
# Scope: ROOT is the child.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.127
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_3
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_wrote_song
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_grateful
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_adore
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_friends
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_brag
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_humble
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_agree
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_disagree
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_impressed
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_1_unimpressed
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_mutual
		clr_character_flag = child_idolizer_version_2_unanswered
	}
}

###############################################################################
# A childhood in a cell
###############################################################################

# childhood.210 Childhood in a cell
# Picks one prison story for a child in the dungeon or the oubliette (not
# house arrest): the rat friend, the Dread King of Darkness, or the prisoner
# next door. These can make the child timid, brooding, indolent or paranoid.
# Scope: ROOT is the imprisoned child. Fired from on_childhood_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.210

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				character_event = { id = childhood.220 days = 10 random = 30 }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_modifier = pet_rat_dungeon }
			}
			10 = { character_event = { id = childhood.230 days = 10 random = 30 } }
			10 = {
				character_event = { id = childhood.240 days = 10 random = 30 }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NAND = {
						age = 6
						OR = {
							has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
							has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
						}
						host = {
							any_courtier = {
								NOT = { character = ROOT }
								NOT = { trait = incapable }
								prisoner = yes
								NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			40 = { # Nothing happens
			}
		}
	}
}

# childhood.220 A rat friend
# A scruffy rat keeps the child company in the cell.
# Scope: ROOT is the child. Fired from childhood.210.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.220
	desc = { text = EVTDESCchildhood220_skaven trigger = { trait = creature_skaven } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCchildhood220 trigger = { NOT = { trait = creature_skaven } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood220 # Name it Flipper
		set_character_flag = rat_name_1
		add_character_modifier = { name = pet_rat_dungeon duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.221 days = 100 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood220 # Name it Cuddles
		set_character_flag = rat_name_2
		add_character_modifier = { name = pet_rat_dungeon duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = childhood.221 days = 100 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCchildhood220 # Name it Smelly
		set_character_flag = rat_name_3
		add_character_modifier = { name = pet_rat_dungeon duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random = {
				chance = 50
				character_event = { id = childhood.221 days = 100 } # Rat gets sick
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDchildhood220 # Eat it
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = indolent } } add_trait = indolent }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_ill = yes } NOT = { trait = pneumonic } }
			random = { chance = 20 add_symptom_effect = yes }
		}
	}
}

# childhood.221 The rat is sick
# The rat falls ill; the child can nurse it, and may catch something.
# Scope: ROOT is the child.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.221
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood221a # Flipper
		trigger = { has_character_flag = rat_name_1 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood221b # Cuddles
		trigger = { has_character_flag = rat_name_2 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood221c # Smelly
		trigger = { has_character_flag = rat_name_3 }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = pet_rat_dungeon
		OR = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = indolent } }
			set_character_flag = childhood221_add_indolent
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = brooding } }
			set_character_flag = childhood221_add_brooding
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood221 # Give it some of my food
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = affectionate } } add_trait = affectionate }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = infirm } trait = pneumonic }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				add_trait = infirm
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38284 #Notify Infirm
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ill = yes }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				add_trait = infection
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = treatment.2 days = 365 random = 1095 } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_ill = yes } NOT = { trait = pneumonic } }
			random = { chance = 30 add_symptom_effect = yes }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood221 # No I need my food
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = childhood221_add_brooding }
			clr_character_flag = childhood221_add_brooding
			add_trait = brooding
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = childhood221_add_indolent }
			clr_character_flag = childhood221_add_indolent
			add_trait = indolent
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = rat_name_1 }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				custom_tooltip = { text = rat_dies_tooltip_1 }
				hidden_tooltip = { remove_character_modifier = pet_rat_dungeon }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = rat_name_2 }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				custom_tooltip = { text = rat_dies_tooltip_2 }
				hidden_tooltip = { remove_character_modifier = pet_rat_dungeon }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = rat_name_3 }
			random = {
				chance = 30
				custom_tooltip = { text = rat_dies_tooltip_3 }
				hidden_tooltip = { remove_character_modifier = pet_rat_dungeon }
			}
		}
	}
}

# childhood.229 Leaving the rat behind
# On release the child must choose whether to leave the rat in the cell.
# Scope: ROOT is the child. Fired from on_release_from_prison and the prison
#   events.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.229
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood229a #
		trigger = { has_character_flag = rat_name_1 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood229b #
		trigger = { has_character_flag = rat_name_2 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood229c #
		trigger = { has_character_flag = rat_name_3 }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = pet_rat_dungeon
	}

	option = {
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAchildhood229 # I will never forget you, rat!
			trigger = { has_character_flag = rat_name_1 }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBchildhood229 # I will never forget you, rat!
			trigger = { has_character_flag = rat_name_2 }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTCchildhood229 # I will never forget you, rat!
			trigger = { has_character_flag = rat_name_3 }
		}
		remove_character_modifier = pet_rat_dungeon
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = brooding } NOT = { age = 12 } } add_trait = brooding }
	}
}

# childhood.231 The three dark stones
# The Dread King sends the child to find three dark stones of sorcery.
# Scope: ROOT is the child.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.231
	desc = EVTDESCchildhood231
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood231 # Use force
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 3 martial = 4 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.3 NOT = { martial = 1 } }
					character_event = { id = childhood.232 days = 50 random = 50 }
				}
				10 = { character_event = { id = childhood.233 days = 50 random = 50 } }
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = dread_king_martial_tooltip }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood231 # Use cunning
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					modifier = { factor = 3 intrigue = 4 }
					modifier = { factor = 0.3 NOT = { intrigue = 1 } }
					character_event = { id = childhood.232 days = 50 random = 50 }
				}
				10 = { character_event = { id = childhood.233 days = 50 random = 50 } }
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = dread_king_intrigue_tooltip }
	}
}

# childhood.240 The prisoner next door
# The child hears another prisoner in the next cell and can reach out.
# Scope: ROOT is the child. Fired from childhood.210.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.240
	desc = EVTDESCchildhood240
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = yes

	trigger = {
		age = 6
		OR = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
		host = {
			any_courtier = {
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { trait = incapable }
				prisoner = yes
				NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		host = {
			random_courtier = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { character = ROOT }
					NOT = { trait = incapable }
					prisoner = yes
					NOT = { is_friend = ROOT }
				}
				save_event_target_as = event_ze_12230_prisoner
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = timid } } set_character_flag = childhood240_add_timid }
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { trait = indolent } }
				set_character_flag = childhood240_add_indolent
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { trait = brooding } }
				set_character_flag = childhood240_add_brooding
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood240 # Talk to them
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:event_ze_12230_prisoner = { character_event = { id = childhood.242 } }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood240 # Ignore them
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = childhood240_add_timid }
			clr_character_flag = childhood240_add_timid
			add_trait = timid
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = childhood240_add_indolent }
			clr_character_flag = childhood240_add_indolent
			add_trait = indolent
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = childhood240_add_brooding }
			clr_character_flag = childhood240_add_brooding
			add_trait = brooding
		}
	}
}

# childhood.242 A voice through the wall
# The other prisoner hears the child calling.
# Scope: ROOT is the other prisoner, FROM the child.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.242
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood242a # Adult
		trigger = { age = 13 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood242b # Child
		trigger = { NOT = { age = 13 } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood242 # Answer
		add_friend = FROM
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = childhood.244 } } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood242 # Ignore
		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_disappointed who = FROM years = 3 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } } add_trait = paranoid }
		else = {
			if = { limit = { trait = paranoid NOT = { trait = shy } } add_trait = shy }
			hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = childhood.246 } } }
		}
	}
}

# childhood.246 Silence
# The other prisoner never answers.
# Scope: ROOT is the child, FROM the other prisoner.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.246
	desc = EVTDESCchildhood246
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = brooding } }
			set_character_flag = childhood246_add_brooding
		}
		else = {
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = timid } } set_character_flag = childhood246_add_timid }
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { trait = indolent } }
				set_character_flag = childhood246_add_indolent
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood246 # Sad
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = childhood246_add_brooding }
			clr_character_flag = childhood246_add_brooding
			add_trait = brooding
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = childhood246_add_timid }
			clr_character_flag = childhood246_add_timid
			add_trait = timid
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = childhood246_add_indolent }
			clr_character_flag = childhood246_add_indolent
			add_trait = indolent
		}
	}
}

# Faith and culture from the educator

# childhood.303 Faith lessons (check)
# Hidden. Checks that the ruler is still the child's educator and of another
# faith, then asks them (childhood.300).
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the child. Fired from childhood.1.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.303
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = { NOT = { FROM = { educator = { character = ROOT } } } religion = FROM }
			}
		}
		else = {
			save_event_target_as = target_educator
			FROM = { save_event_target_as = target_child }
			character_event = { id = childhood.300 }
		}
	}
}

# childhood.300 Faith lessons
# The educator may raise the child in the educator's own faith.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator; event targets target_child and
#   target_educator.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.300
	desc = EVTDESCchildhood300
	picture = GFX_evt_guardian
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood300
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		tooltip = { reverse_religion = event_target:target_child }
		event_target:target_child = { character_event = { id = childhood.301 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood300
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# childhood.301 A new faith
# The child is asked to take the educator's faith.
# Scope: ROOT is the child.
character_event = {
	id = childhood.301
	desc = EVTDESCchildhood301
	picture = GFX_evt_guardian
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { religion = event_target:target_educator }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood301
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		religion = event_target:target_educator
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					liege = { NOR = { character = event_target:target_educator character = ROOT } }
				}
				liege = { character_event = { id = childhood.302 } }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood301
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		prestige = -5
	}
}

	}

# childhood.330 Asking leave to teach a ward
# The guardian of a ward asks the ward's liege for leave to raise the ward
# in the guardian's faith and culture (the heritage focus). The AI always
# agrees.
# Scope: ROOT is the liege, FROM the ward, FROMFROM the guardian. Fired from
#   decisions/ze_decisions.txt.
letter_event = {
	id = childhood.330
	desc = EVTDESCchildhood330
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	show_from_from = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = target_liege
		FROM = { save_event_target_as = target_ward }
		FROMFROM = { save_event_target_as = target_guardian }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood330
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100 # AI always accepts
		}

		tooltip = { event_target:target_ward = { set_focus = focus_ch_heritage } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:target_guardian = { letter_event = { id = childhood.331 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood330
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { opinion = { who = event_target:target_guardian value = 25 } }
			tooltip = {
				reverse_opinion = {
					who = event_target:target_guardian
					modifier = opinion_insulted
					years = 2
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:target_guardian = { letter_event = { id = childhood.332 } }
		}
	}
}

# childhood.331 Leave given
# The guardian hears yes and the ward turns to the heritage focus.
# Scope: ROOT is the guardian.
letter_event = {
	id = childhood.331
	desc = EVTDESCchildhood331
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood331
		event_target:target_ward = {
			hidden_tooltip = { clear_focus = yes }
			set_focus = focus_ch_heritage
		}
	}
}

# childhood.332 Leave refused
# The guardian hears no, politely or with an insult, depending on the
# liege's opinion.
# Scope: ROOT is the guardian.
letter_event = {
	id = childhood.332
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood332a
		trigger = { has_character_flag = childhood332_diplomatic_response }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood332b
		trigger = { has_character_flag = childhood332_offended_response }
	}
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { reverse_opinion = { who = event_target:target_liege value = 25 } }
			set_character_flag = childhood332_diplomatic_response
		}
		else = { set_character_flag = childhood332_offended_response }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood332
		trigger = { has_character_flag = childhood332_diplomatic_response }
		clr_character_flag = childhood332_diplomatic_response
		clr_character_flag = childhood332_offended_response
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood332
		trigger = { has_character_flag = childhood332_offended_response }
		opinion = { who = event_target:target_liege modifier = opinion_insulted years = 2 }
		clr_character_flag = childhood332_diplomatic_response
		clr_character_flag = childhood332_offended_response
	}
}

# childhood.342 Focus change refused
# The guardian hears no.
# Scope: ROOT is the guardian.
letter_event = {
	id = childhood.342
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood332a
		trigger = { has_character_flag = childhood342_diplomatic_response }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCchildhood342b
		trigger = { has_character_flag = childhood342_offended_response }
	}
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { reverse_opinion = { who = event_target:target_liege value = 25 } }
			set_character_flag = childhood342_diplomatic_response
		}
		else = { set_character_flag = childhood342_offended_response }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAchildhood332
		trigger = { has_character_flag = childhood342_diplomatic_response }
		clr_character_flag = childhood342_diplomatic_response
		clr_character_flag = childhood342_offended_response
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBchildhood332
		trigger = { has_character_flag = childhood342_offended_response }
		opinion = { who = event_target:target_liege modifier = opinion_insulted years = 2 }
		clr_character_flag = childhood342_diplomatic_response
		clr_character_flag = childhood342_offended_response
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 2: adolescence
#
# Namespace: adolescence (was ZE.13000-13999 and ZE.40000 in
# ze_adolescence_events.txt).
#
#   adolescence.1-4     pulses and clean-up
#   adolescence.10-124  each childhood trait grows up, a block of ten per trait
#                       as in Part 1 (+0 hidden pick, +1 the change, +2 the
#                       educator steps in, +4 the educator's verdict)
#   adolescence.3, 200-254  first love
#   adolescence.205-215 the educator's influence
###############################################################################

namespace = adolescence

###############################################################################
# Adolescence: pulses and clean-up
###############################################################################

# adolescence.1 The adolescence pulse
# Hidden. Picks which of the child's childhood traits matures next
# (adolescence.10-120).
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse (12 and
#   14).
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.1

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		is_adult = no
		age = 12
		OR = {
			trait = haughty
			trait = affectionate
			trait = timid
			trait = rowdy
			trait = willful
			trait = brooding
			trait = indolent
			trait = playful
			trait = conscientious
			trait = fussy
			trait = curious
			trait = idolizer
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = haughty } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = { OR = { trait = proud trait = arbitrary trait = cruel } }
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.10 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = affectionate } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = { OR = { trait = kind trait = content trait = trusting } }
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.20 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = timid } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = { OR = { trait = shy trait = humble trait = craven } }
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.30 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = rowdy } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = { OR = { trait = robust trait = honest trait = dull } }
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.40 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = willful } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = { OR = { trait = brave trait = ambitious trait = stubborn } }
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.50 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = brooding } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = { OR = { trait = just trait = wroth trait = envious } }
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.60 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = indolent } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = {
						OR = {
							trait = charitable
							trait = slothful
							trait = gluttonous
							trait = sturdy
							trait = is_fat
						}
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.70 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = playful } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = {
						OR = {
							trait = gregarious
							trait = deceitful
							trait = lunatic_1
							trait = lunatic_2
							trait = lunatic_3
						}
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.80 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = conscientious } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = {
						OR = {
							trait = diligent
							trait = temperate
							trait = feeble
							trait = is_malnourished
						}
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.90 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = fussy } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = { OR = { trait = patient trait = paranoid trait = greedy } }
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.100 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = curious } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = { OR = { trait = cynical trait = shrewd } }
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.110 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = idolizer } }
				modifier = { #Monkey sees...
					factor = 1.45
					educator = {
						OR = {
							trait = zealous
							trait = erudite
							trait = feeble
							trait = is_malnourished
						}
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.120 days = 10 random = 90 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.2 Chain flags cleared
# Clears the adolescence chain flags.
# Scope: ROOT is the new adult. Fired from on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.2

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		clr_character_flag = child_became_less_smart
		clr_character_flag = child_became_smarter
	}
}

# adolescence.4 Childhood traits left over
# Hidden. Turns any childhood trait still held at adulthood into an adult
# one, for incapable children and others the chains missed.
# Scope: ROOT is the new adult. Fired from on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.4
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { trait = indolent }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = charitable }
				1 = { add_trait = slothful }
				1 = { add_trait = gluttonous }
			}
			remove_trait = indolent
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = playful }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = gregarious }
				1 = { add_trait = deceitful }
				1 = { add_trait = lunatic_1 set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 5 } }
			}
			remove_trait = playful
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = affectionate }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = kind }
				1 = { add_trait = content }
				1 = { add_trait = trusting }
			}
			remove_trait = affectionate
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = timid }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = humble }
				1 = { add_trait = shy }
				1 = { add_trait = craven }
			}
			remove_trait = timid
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = brooding }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = just }
				1 = { add_trait = wroth }
				1 = { add_trait = envious }
			}
			remove_trait = brooding
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = haughty }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = proud }
				1 = { add_trait = arbitrary }
				1 = { add_trait = cruel }
			}
			remove_trait = haughty
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = rowdy }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = robust }
				1 = { add_trait = honest }
				1 = { add_trait = dull }
			}
			remove_trait = rowdy
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = willful }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = ambitious }
				1 = { add_trait = brave }
				1 = { add_trait = stubborn }
			}
			remove_trait = willful
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = conscientious }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = diligent }
				1 = { add_trait = temperate }
			}
			remove_Trait = conscientious
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = curious }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = cynical }
				1 = { add_trait = shrewd }
			}
			remove_trait = curious
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = fussy }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = patient }
				1 = { add_trait = greedy }
				1 = { add_trait = paranoid }
			}
			remove_trait = fussy
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = idolizer }
			random_list = {
				1 = { add_trait = zealous }
				1 = { add_trait = erudite }
				1 = { add_trait = feeble }
			}
			remove_trait = idolizer
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Adolescence: childhood traits grow up
###############################################################################

# adolescence.10 Haughty grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the haughty trait becomes: proud, arbitrary, cruel or
# groomed, weighted by traits, focus and the educator. A ambitious educator
# may step in first (adolescence.12); otherwise the change comes at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.10

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = haughty
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_haughty
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = haughty_to_proud
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = humble trait = proud } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = haughty_to_arbitrary
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = just trait = arbitrary } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = haughty_to_cruel
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = kind trait = cruel } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = haughty_to_groomed
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = groomed trait = uncouth } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = proud } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_focus = focus_ch_pride }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 has_focus = focus_ch_etiquette }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 has_focus = focus_ed_diplomacy }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					is_female = yes
					NOT = { educator = { has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal } }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = { trait = humble trait = proud }
					OR = { trait = just trait = arbitrary }
					OR = { trait = kind trait = cruel }
					OR = { trait = groomed trait = uncouth }
				}
			}
			change_martial = 2
			remove_trait = haughty
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = ambitious } educator = { trait = ambitious } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.12 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.11 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.11 Haughty: the change
# The haughty trait matures into the picked trait.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.11
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence11a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_proud } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence11b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_arbitrary } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence11c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_cruel } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCDHFP20800_HAUGHTY_GROOMED
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_groomed } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence11
		trigger = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_proud }
		remove_trait = haughty
		add_trait = proud
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_proud
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence11
		trigger = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_arbitrary }
		remove_trait = haughty
		add_trait = arbitrary
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_arbitrary
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence11
		trigger = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_cruel }
		remove_trait = haughty
		add_trait = cruel
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_cruel
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAHFP20800_HAUGHTY_GROOMED
		trigger = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_groomed }
		remove_trait = haughty
		add_trait = groomed
		balance_weight_small_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_groomed
	}
}

# adolescence.12 Haughty: the educator steps in
# A ambitious educator may make the child ambitious instead.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.12
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence12a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_proud } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence12b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_arbitrary } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence12c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_cruel } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCDHFP20800_HAUGHTY_GROOMED_ED
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_groomed } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence12 # The child will be like me
		FROM = { custom_tooltip = { text = haughty_may_become_ambitious } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.14 } } }
		show_trait = ambitious
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence12 # Do not intervene
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.11 } } } # Go to normal trait change
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_proud } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = haughty add_trait = proud } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_arbitrary } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = haughty add_trait = arbitrary } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_cruel } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = haughty add_trait = cruel } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_groomed } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = haughty add_trait = groomed } }
		}
	}
}

	only_capable = yes

		name = EVTOPTAadolescence14
		remove_trait = haughty
		add_trait = ambitious
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_proud
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_arbitrary
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_cruel
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_groomed
		if = {
			limit = { educator = { ai = no } }
			educator = { set_character_flag = achievement_follow_me }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence14
		trigger = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_proud }
		remove_trait = haughty
		add_trait = proud
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_proud
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence14
		trigger = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_arbitrary }
		remove_trait = haughty
		add_trait = arbitrary
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_arbitrary
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDadolescence14
		trigger = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_cruel }
		remove_trait = haughty
		add_trait = cruel
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_cruel
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAHFP20800_HAUGHTY_GROOMED_RESP
		trigger = { has_character_flag = haughty_to_groomed }
		remove_trait = haughty
		add_trait = groomed
		balance_weight_small_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = haughty_to_groomed
	}
}

# adolescence.20 Affectionate grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the affectionate trait becomes: kind, content or
# trusting, weighted by traits, focus and the educator. A charitable
# educator may step in first (adolescence.22); otherwise the change comes at
# once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.20

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = affectionate
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_affectionate
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = affectionate_to_kind
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = kind trait = cruel trait = envious } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = kind } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = affectionate_to_content
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = content trait = ambitious } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = content } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = affectionate_to_trusting
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = trusting trait = paranoid } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = trusting } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = { trait = kind trait = cruel trait = envious }
					OR = { trait = content trait = ambitious }
					OR = { trait = trusting trait = paranoid }
				}
			}
			change_diplomacy = 2
			remove_trait = affectionate
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = charitable } educator = { trait = charitable } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.22 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.21 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.22 Affectionate: the educator steps in

character_event = {
	id = adolescence.22
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence22a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = affectionate_to_kind } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence22b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = affectionate_to_content } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence22c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = affectionate_to_trusting } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

		show_trait = charitable
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence22 # Do not intervene
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.21 } } } # Go to normal trait change
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = affectionate_to_kind } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = affectionate add_trait = kind } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = affectionate_to_content } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = affectionate add_trait = content } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = affectionate_to_trusting } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = affectionate add_trait = trusting } }
		}
	}
}

		name = EVTOPTBadolescence24
		trigger = { has_character_flag = affectionate_to_kind }
		remove_trait = affectionate

		clr_character_flag = affectionate_to_content
	}

		name = EVTOPTDadolescence24
		trigger = { has_character_flag = affectionate_to_trusting }
		remove_trait = affectionate
		add_trait = trusting
		clr_character_flag = affectionate_to_trusting
	}
}

# adolescence.30 Timid grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the timid trait becomes: shy, humble or craven,
# weighted by traits, focus and the educator. A content educator may step in
# first (adolescence.32); otherwise the change comes at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.30

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = timid
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_timid
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = timid_to_humble
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = humble trait = proud } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = humble } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = timid_to_shy
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = shy trait = gregarious } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = shy } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = timid_to_craven
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = craven trait = brave } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = craven } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = { trait = humble trait = proud }
					OR = { trait = shy trait = gregarious }
					OR = { trait = craven trait = brave }
				}
			}
			change_learning = 2
			remove_trait = timid
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = content } educator = { trait = content } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.32 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.31 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

	}

		text = EVTDESCadolescence31c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = timid_to_craven } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

# adolescence.32 Timid: the educator steps in
# A content educator may make the child content instead.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.32
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence32a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = timid_to_humble } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence32b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = timid_to_shy } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence32c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = timid_to_craven } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence32 # The child will be like me
		FROM = { custom_tooltip = { text = timid_may_become_content } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.34 } } }
		show_trait = content
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence32 # Do not intervene
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.31 } } } # Go to normal trait change
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = timid_to_humble } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = timid add_trait = humble } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = timid_to_shy } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = timid add_trait = shy } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = timid_to_craven } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = timid add_trait = craven } }
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.40 Rowdy grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the rowdy trait becomes: strong, honest, uncouth or
# imbecile, weighted by traits, focus and the educator. A brave educator may
# step in first (adolescence.42); otherwise the change comes at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.40

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = rowdy
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_rowdy
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = rowdy_to_strong
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { is_strong_trigger = yes is_weak_trigger = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = robust } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = rowdy_to_honest
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = honest trait = deceitful } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = honest } }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = { is_dumb_trigger = yes has_character_flag = child_became_smarter }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.35 educator = { trait = slow } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.65 educator = { trait = imbecile } }
				set_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile
				set_character_flag = child_became_less_smart
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = rowdy_to_uncouth
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = groomed trait = uncouth } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = shy } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_focus = focus_ed_martial }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = { is_strong_trigger = yes is_weak_trigger = yes }
					OR = { trait = honest trait = deceitful }
					OR = { is_dumb_trigger = yes has_character_flag = child_became_smarter }
					OR = { trait = groomed trait = uncouth }
				}
			}
			change_martial = 2
			remove_trait = rowdy
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = brave } educator = { trait = brave } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.42 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.41 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.41 Rowdy: the change
# The rowdy trait matures into the picked trait.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.41
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence41a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_strong } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence41b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_honest } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence41c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCDHFP20800_ROWDY_UNCOUTH
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_uncouth } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile trait = genius }
			set_character_flag = rowdy_genius_to_quick
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile trait = quick }
			set_character_flag = rowdy_quick_to_normal
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile trait = slow }
				set_character_flag = rowdy_slow_to_imbecile
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile }
				set_character_flag = rowdy_normal_to_slow
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence41
		trigger = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_strong }
		remove_trait = rowdy
		add_trait = robust
		clr_character_flag = rowdy_to_strong
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence41
		trigger = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_honest }
		remove_trait = rowdy
		add_trait = honest
		clr_character_flag = rowdy_to_honest
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence41
		trigger = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile }
		clr_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = rowdy_genius_to_quick }
			remove_trait = rowdy
			remove_trait = genius
			add_trait = quick
			clr_character_flag = rowdy_genius_to_quick
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = rowdy_quick_to_normal }
			remove_trait = rowdy
			remove_trait = quick
			clr_character_flag = rowdy_quick_to_normal
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = rowdy_normal_to_slow }
			remove_trait = rowdy
			add_trait = dull
			clr_character_flag = rowdy_normal_to_slow
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = rowdy_slow_to_imbecile }
			remove_trait = rowdy
			add_trait = dull
			clr_character_flag = rowdy_slow_to_imbecile
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDHFP20800_ROWDY_UNCOUTH
		trigger = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_uncouth }
		remove_trait = rowdy
		add_trait = uncouth
		clr_character_flag = rowdy_to_uncouth
	}
}

# adolescence.42 Rowdy: the educator steps in
# A brave educator may make the child brave instead.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.42
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence42a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_strong } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence42b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_honest } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence42c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCEHFP20800_ROWDY_UNCOUTH_ED
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_uncouth } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence42 # The child will be like me
		FROM = { custom_tooltip = { text = rowdy_may_become_brave } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.44 } } }
		show_trait = brave
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence42 # Do not intervene
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.41 } } } # Go to normal trait change
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_strong } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = rowdy add_trait = robust } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_honest } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = rowdy add_trait = honest } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_uncouth } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = rowdy add_trait = uncouth } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile trait = genius } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = rowdy remove_trait = genius add_trait = quick } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile trait = quick } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = rowdy remove_trait = quick } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile trait = slow } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = rowdy remove_trait = slow add_trait = imbecile } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					has_character_flag = rowdy_to_imbecile
					NOT = { trait = genius }
					NOT = { trait = quick }
					NOT = { trait = slow }
				}
			}
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = rowdy add_trait = dull } }
		}
	}
}
