# adolescence.50 Willful grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the willful trait becomes: ambitious, brave or
# stubborn, weighted by traits, focus and the educator. A proud educator may
# step in first (adolescence.52); otherwise the change comes at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.50

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = willful
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_willful
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = willful_to_ambitious
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = content trait = ambitious } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = ambitious } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = willful_to_brave
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = craven trait = brave } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = brave } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = willful_to_stubborn
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = stubborn }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = stubborn } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = { trait = content trait = ambitious }
					OR = { trait = craven trait = brave }
					trait = stubborn
				}
			}
			change_intrigue = 2
			remove_trait = willful
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = proud } educator = { trait = proud } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.52 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.51 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence52c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = willful_to_stubborn } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence52 # The child will be like me
		FROM = { custom_tooltip = { text = willful_may_become_proud } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.54 } } }
		show_trait = proud
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence52_b # Do not intervene
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.51 } } } # Go to normal trait change

			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = willful_to_ambitious } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = willful add_trait = ambitious } }
		}
		if = {

		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = willful_to_stubborn } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = willful add_trait = stubborn } }
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.54 Willful: the educator's verdict

	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

		text = EVTDESCadolescence54a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = willful_to_ambitious } }

	}

		text = EVTDESCadolescence54c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = willful_to_stubborn } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence54
		remove_trait = willful
		add_trait = proud
		clr_character_flag = willful_to_ambitious
		clr_character_flag = willful_to_brave
		clr_character_flag = willful_to_stubborn
		if = {
			limit = { educator = { ai = no } }
			educator = { set_character_flag = achievement_follow_me }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence54
		trigger = { has_character_flag = willful_to_ambitious }
		remove_trait = willful
		add_trait = ambitious
		clr_character_flag = willful_to_ambitious
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence54
		trigger = { has_character_flag = willful_to_brave }
		remove_trait = willful
		add_trait = brave
		clr_character_flag = willful_to_brave
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDadolescence54
		trigger = { has_character_flag = willful_to_stubborn }
		remove_trait = willful
		add_trait = stubborn
		clr_character_flag = willful_to_stubborn
	}
}

# adolescence.60 Brooding grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the brooding trait becomes: just, wroth, envious or
# uncouth, weighted by traits, focus and the educator. A temperate educator
# may step in first (adolescence.62); otherwise the change comes at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.60

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = brooding
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_brooding
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = brooding_to_just
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = arbitrary trait = just } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = just } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = brooding_to_wroth
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = patient trait = wroth } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = wroth } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = brooding_to_envious
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = kind trait = envious } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = envious } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = brooding_to_uncouth
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = groomed trait = uncouth } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = shy } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_focus = focus_ed_martial }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = { trait = arbitrary trait = just }
					OR = { trait = patient trait = wroth }
					OR = { trait = kind trait = envious }
					OR = { trait = groomed trait = uncouth }
				}
			}
			change_intrigue = 2
			remove_trait = brooding
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = temperate } educator = { trait = temperate } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.62 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.61 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.70 Indolent grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the indolent trait becomes: charitable, slothful,
# gluttonous, sturdy or groomed, weighted by traits, focus and the educator.
# A gregarious educator may step in first (adolescence.72); otherwise the
# change comes at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.70

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = indolent
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_indolent
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = indolent_to_charitable
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = greedy trait = charitable } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = charitable } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = indolent_to_slothful
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = diligent trait = slothful } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = slothful } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = indolent_to_gluttonous
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = temperate trait = gluttonous } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = gluttonous } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = indolent_to_sturdy
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = sturdy trait = feeble trait = robust } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					educator = { OR = { trait = gluttonous trait = sturdy trait = is_fat } }
				}
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = indolent_to_groomed
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = groomed trait = uncouth } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = proud } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_focus = focus_ch_pride }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 has_focus = focus_ch_etiquette }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 has_focus = focus_ed_diplomacy }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					is_female = yes
					NOT = { educator = { has_religion_feature = religion_matriarchal } }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = { trait = greedy trait = charitable }
					OR = { trait = diligent trait = slothful }
					OR = { trait = temperate trait = gluttonous }
					OR = { trait = sturdy trait = feeble }
					OR = { trait = groomed trait = uncouth }
				}
			}
			change_intrigue = 2
			remove_trait = indolent
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = gregarious } educator = { trait = gregarious } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.72 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.71 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

			educator = { set_character_flag = achievement_follow_me }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence74
		trigger = { has_character_flag = indolent_to_charitable }
		remove_trait = indolent
		add_trait = charitable
		clr_character_flag = indolent_to_charitable
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence74
		trigger = { has_character_flag = indolent_to_slothful }
		remove_trait = indolent
		add_trait = slothful
		clr_character_flag = indolent_to_slothful
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDadolescence74
		trigger = { has_character_flag = indolent_to_gluttonous }
		remove_trait = indolent
		add_trait = gluttonous
		gain_weight_small_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = indolent_to_gluttonous
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTEHFP20669
		trigger = { has_character_flag = indolent_to_sturdy }
		remove_trait = indolent
		add_trait = sturdy
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				70 = { modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = content } }
				30 = { character_event = { id = physique.120 days = 900 random = 400 } } #Change physical state event
			}
		}
		gain_weight_small_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = indolent_to_sturdy
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTFHFP20800_INDOLENT_GROOMED
		trigger = { has_character_flag = indolent_to_groomed }
		remove_trait = indolent
		add_trait = groomed
		balance_weight_small_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = indolent_to_groomed
	}
}

# adolescence.80 Playful grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the playful trait becomes: gregarious, deceitful or
# lunatic, weighted by traits, focus and the educator. A cruel educator may
# step in first (adolescence.82); otherwise the change comes at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.80

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = playful
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_playful
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			15 = {
				set_character_flag = playful_to_gregarious
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = gregarious }
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = shy }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = gregarious } }
			}
			15 = {
				set_character_flag = playful_to_deceitful
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = deceitful }
				modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = honest }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = deceitful } }
			}
			1 = {
				set_character_flag = playful_to_lunatic # This should be RARE but affected by modifiers/traumas
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 3 trait = genius }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					educator = {
						OR = {
							trait = possessed
							trait = lunatic_1
							trait = lunatic_2
							trait = lunatic_3
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			# This combination should not be possible, but just to be sure...
			limit = {
				trait = gregarious
				trait = deceitful
				OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
			}
			change_diplomacy = 2
			remove_trait = playful
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } educator = { trait = cruel } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.82 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.81 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.81 Playful: the change
# The playful trait matures into the picked trait.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.81
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence81a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = playful_to_gregarious } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence81b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = playful_to_deceitful } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence81c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = playful_to_lunatic } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence81
		trigger = { has_character_flag = playful_to_gregarious }
		remove_trait = playful
		add_trait = gregarious
		clr_character_flag = playful_to_gregarious
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence81
		trigger = { has_character_flag = playful_to_deceitful }
		remove_trait = playful
		add_trait = deceitful
		clr_character_flag = playful_to_deceitful
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence81
		trigger = { has_character_flag = playful_to_lunatic }
		remove_trait = playful
		add_trait = lunatic_1
		set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 5 }
		clr_character_flag = playful_to_lunatic
	}
}

# adolescence.82 Playful: the educator steps in
# A cruel educator may make the child cruel instead.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.82
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence82a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = playful_to_gregarious } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence82b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = playful_to_deceitful } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence82c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = playful_to_lunatic } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence82 # The child will be like me
		FROM = { custom_tooltip = { text = playful_may_become_cruel } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.84 } } }
		show_trait = cruel
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence82 # Do not intervene
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.81 } } } # Go to normal trait change
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = playful_to_gregarious } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = playful add_trait = gregarious } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = playful_to_deceitful } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = playful add_trait = deceitful } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = playful_to_lunatic } }
			tooltip = {
				FROM = {
					remove_trait = playful
					add_trait = lunatic_1
					set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 5 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.90 Conscientious grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the conscientious trait becomes: diligent or temperate,
# weighted by traits, focus and the educator. A just educator may step in
# first (adolescence.92); otherwise the change comes at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.90

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = conscientious
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_conscientious
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = conscientious_to_diligent
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = slothful trait = diligent } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = diligent } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = conscientious_to_temperate
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = gluttonous trait = temperate } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = temperate } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = { trait = slothful trait = diligent }
					OR = { trait = gluttonous trait = temperate }
				}
			}
			change_stewardship = 2
			remove_trait = conscientious
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = just } educator = { trait = just } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.92 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.91 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.91 Conscientious: the change
# The conscientious trait matures into the picked trait.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.91
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence91a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = conscientious_to_diligent } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence91b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = conscientious_to_temperate } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence91
		trigger = { has_character_flag = conscientious_to_diligent }
		remove_trait = conscientious
		add_trait = diligent
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			random = {
				chance = 20
				add_trait = stressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38282 #Notify Stressed
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = conscientious_to_diligent
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence91
		trigger = { has_character_flag = conscientious_to_temperate }
		remove_trait = conscientious
		add_trait = temperate
		lose_weight_small_effect = yes
		weight_trait_check_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			random = {
				chance = 20
				add_trait = stressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = 38282 #Notify Stressed
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = conscientious_to_temperate
	}
}

# adolescence.100 Fussy grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the fussy trait becomes: patient, greedy or paranoid,
# weighted by traits, focus and the educator. A diligent educator may step
# in first (adolescence.102); otherwise the change comes at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.100

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = fussy
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_fussy
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = fussy_to_patient
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = wroth trait = patient } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = patient } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = fussy_to_greedy
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = charitable trait = greedy } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = greedy } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = fussy_to_paranoid
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = trusting trait = paranoid } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = paranoid } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = { trait = wroth trait = patient }
					OR = { trait = charitable trait = greedy }
					OR = { trait = trusting trait = paranoid }
				}
			}
			change_stewardship = 2
			remove_trait = fussy
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = diligent } educator = { trait = diligent } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.102 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.101 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.110 Curious grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the curious trait becomes: genius or cynical, weighted
# by traits, focus and the educator. A patient educator may step in first
# (adolescence.112); otherwise the change comes at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.110

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = curious
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_curious
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = curious_to_cynical
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = zealous trait = cynical } }
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						NOR = {
							educator = { NOT = { religion_group = ROOT } }
							location = { NOT = { religion_group = ROOT } }
							any_friend = { NOT = { religion_group = ROOT } }
							any_sibling = { NOT = { religion_group = ROOT } }
						}
						trait = cynical
						trait = sympathy_old_world_group
						trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_cadai_group
						trait = sympathy_cytharai_group
						trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_halfling_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = curious_sympathy_religion
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = { trait = shrewd has_character_flag = child_became_less_smart }
				}
				set_character_flag = curious_to_genius
				set_character_flag = child_became_smarter
			}
		}

		# If sympathy for religion, choose which one...
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = curious_sympathy_religion }
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					set_character_flag = add_sympathy_old_world_group
					modifier = { factor = 0 religion_group = old_world_group }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOR = {
							educator = { religion_group = old_world_group }
							location = { religion_group = old_world_group }
							any_friend = { religion_group = old_world_group }
							any_sibling = { religion_group = old_world_group }
						}
					}
				}
				10 = {
					set_character_flag = add_sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
					modifier = { factor = 0 religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOR = {
							educator = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
							location = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
							any_friend = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
							any_sibling = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
						}
					}
				}
				10 = {
					set_character_flag = add_sympathy_cadai_group
					modifier = { factor = 0 religion_group = cadai_group }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOR = {
							educator = { religion_group = cadai_group }
							location = { religion_group = cadai_group }
							any_friend = { religion_group = cadai_group }
							any_sibling = { religion_group = cadai_group }
						}
					}
				}
				10 = {
					set_character_flag = add_sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
					modifier = { factor = 0 religion_group = dwarf_gods_group }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOR = {
							educator = { religion_group = dwarf_gods_group }
							location = { religion_group = dwarf_gods_group }
							any_friend = { religion_group = dwarf_gods_group }
							any_sibling = { religion_group = dwarf_gods_group }
						}
					}
				}
				10 = {
					set_character_flag = add_sympathy_cytharai_group
					modifier = { factor = 0 religion_group = cytharai_group }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOR = {
							educator = { religion_group = cytharai_group }
							location = { religion_group = cytharai_group }
							any_friend = { religion_group = cytharai_group }
							any_sibling = { religion_group = cytharai_group }
						}
					}
				}
				10 = {
					set_character_flag = add_sympathy_halfling_gods_group
					modifier = { factor = 0 religion_group = halfling_gods_group }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOR = {
							educator = { religion_group = halfling_gods_group }
							location = { religion_group = halfling_gods_group }
							any_friend = { religion_group = halfling_gods_group }
							any_sibling = { religion_group = halfling_gods_group }
						}
					}
				}
				10 = {
					set_character_flag = add_sympathy_oriental_gods_group
					modifier = { factor = 0 religion_group = oriental_gods_group }
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOR = {
							educator = { religion_group = oriental_gods_group }
							location = { religion_group = oriental_gods_group }
							any_friend = { religion_group = oriental_gods_group }
							any_sibling = { religion_group = oriental_gods_group }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = { trait = zealous trait = cynical }
					OR = {
						NOR = {
							educator = { NOT = { religion_group = ROOT } }
							location = { NOT = { religion_group = ROOT } }
							any_friend = { NOT = { religion_group = ROOT } }
							any_sibling = { NOT = { religion_group = ROOT } }
						}
						trait = cynical
						trait = sympathy_old_world_group
						trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_cadai_group
						trait = sympathy_cytharai_group
						trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_halfling_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
					}
					OR = { trait = shrewd has_character_flag = child_became_less_smart }
				}
			}
			change_stewardship = 2
			remove_trait = curious
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = patient } educator = { trait = patient } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.112 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.111 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.111 Curious: the change
# The curious trait matures into the picked trait.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.111
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence111a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_to_cynical } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence111b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_sympathy_religion } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence111c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius trait = imbecile }
			set_character_flag = curious_imbecile_to_slow
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius trait = slow }
				set_character_flag = curious_slow_to_normal
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius is_dumb_trigger = no }
				set_character_flag = curious_normal_to_shrewd
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence111
		trigger = { has_character_flag = curious_to_cynical }
		remove_trait = curious
		add_trait = cynical
		clr_character_flag = curious_to_cynical
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence111
		trigger = { has_character_flag = curious_sympathy_religion }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_old_world_group }
			remove_trait = curious
			add_trait = sympathy_old_world_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_old_world_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_ormazd_gods_group }
			remove_trait = curious
			add_trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_cadai_group }
			remove_trait = curious
			add_trait = sympathy_cadai_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_cadai_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_cytharai_group }
			remove_trait = curious
			add_trait = sympathy_cytharai_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_cytharai_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_dwarf_gods_group }
			remove_trait = curious
			add_trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_halfling_gods_group }
			remove_trait = curious
			add_trait = sympathy_halfling_gods_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_halfling_gods_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_oriental_gods_group }
			remove_trait = curious
			add_trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_oriental_gods_group
		}
		clr_character_flag = curious_sympathy_religion
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence111
		trigger = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius }
		clr_character_flag = curious_to_genius
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = curious_imbecile_to_slow }
			remove_trait = curious
			remove_trait = imbecile
			add_trait = slow
			clr_character_flag = curious_imbecile_to_slow
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = curious_slow_to_normal }
			remove_trait = curious
			remove_trait = slow
			clr_character_flag = curious_slow_to_normal
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = curious_imbecile_to_slow
					has_character_flag = curious_slow_to_normal
				}
			}
			remove_trait = curious
			add_trait = shrewd
			clr_character_flag = curious_normal_to_shrewd
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.112 Curious: the educator steps in
# A patient educator may make the child patient instead.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.112
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence112a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_to_cynical } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence112b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_sympathy_religion } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence112c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence112 # The child will be like me
		FROM = { custom_tooltip = { text = curious_may_become_patient } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.114 } } }
		show_trait = patient
	}
	option = {
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTBadolescence112 # Do not intervene
			trigger = { FROM = { is_female = no } }
		}
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTCadolescence112 # Do not intervene
			trigger = { FROM = { is_female = yes } }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.111 } } } # Go to normal trait change
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_to_cynical } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious add_trait = cynical } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_old_world_group } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious add_trait = sympathy_old_world_group } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_ormazd_gods_group } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious add_trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_cadai_group } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious add_trait = sympathy_cadai_group } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_cytharai_group } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious add_trait = sympathy_cytharai_group } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_dwarf_gods_group } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious add_trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_halfling_gods_group } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious add_trait = sympathy_halfling_gods_group } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_oriental_gods_group } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious add_trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius trait = imbecile } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious remove_trait = imbecile add_trait = slow } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius trait = slow } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious remove_trait = slow } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					has_character_flag = curious_to_genius
					OR = { trait = quick trait = genius }
				}
			}
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious add_trait = shrewd } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					has_character_flag = curious_to_genius
					NOT = { trait = quick }
					NOT = { trait = slow }
					NOT = { trait = imbecile }
					NOT = { trait = genius }
				}
			}
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = curious add_trait = shrewd } }
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.114 Curious: the educator's verdict
# Tells the child what the educator's guidance came to.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent, FROM the educator.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.114
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence114a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_to_cynical } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence114b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_sympathy_religion } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence114c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius trait = imbecile }
			set_character_flag = curious_imbecile_to_slow
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius trait = slow }
				set_character_flag = curious_slow_to_normal
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius is_dumb_trigger = no }
				set_character_flag = curious_normal_to_shrewd
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence114
		remove_trait = curious
		add_trait = patient
		clr_character_flag = curious_to_cynical
		clr_character_flag = curious_sympathy_religion
		clr_character_flag = curious_to_genius
		if = {
			limit = { educator = { ai = no } }
			educator = { set_character_flag = achievement_follow_me }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence114
		trigger = { has_character_flag = curious_to_cynical }
		remove_trait = curious
		add_trait = cynical
		clr_character_flag = curious_to_cynical
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence114
		trigger = { has_character_flag = curious_sympathy_religion }
		remove_trait = curious
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_old_world_group }
			add_trait = sympathy_old_world_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_old_world_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_ormazd_gods_group }
			add_trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_cadai_group }
			add_trait = sympathy_cadai_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_cadai_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_cytharai_group }
			add_trait = sympathy_cytharai_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_cytharai_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_dwarf_gods_group }
			add_trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_halfling_gods_group }
			add_trait = sympathy_halfling_gods_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_halfling_gods_group
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = add_sympathy_oriental_gods_group }
			add_trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
			clr_character_flag = add_sympathy_oriental_gods_group
		}
		clr_character_flag = curious_sympathy_religion
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDadolescence114
		trigger = { has_character_flag = curious_to_genius }
		clr_character_flag = curious_to_genius
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = curious_imbecile_to_slow }
			remove_trait = curious
			remove_trait = imbecile
			add_trait = slow
			clr_character_flag = curious_imbecile_to_slow
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = curious_slow_to_normal }
			remove_trait = curious
			remove_trait = slow
			clr_character_flag = curious_slow_to_normal
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = curious_normal_to_shrewd }
			remove_trait = curious
			add_trait = shrewd
			clr_character_flag = curious_normal_to_shrewd
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.120 Idolizer grows up
# Hidden. Picks what the idolizer trait becomes: zealous, erudite or weak,
# weighted by traits, focus and the educator. A kind educator may step in
# first (adolescence.122); otherwise the change comes at once.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse and
#   on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.120

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = idolizer
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = was_idolizer
		random_list = { # Select outcome
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = idolizer_to_zealous
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						trait = cynical
						trait = zealous
						trait = sympathy_old_world_group
						trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_cadai_group
						trait = sympathy_cytharai_group
						trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_halfling_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = zealous religion = ROOT } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = idolizer_to_erudite
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = erudite }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { trait = erudite } }
			}
			10 = {
				set_character_flag = idolizer_to_weak
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { is_strong_trigger = yes is_weak_trigger = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 educator = { is_weak_trigger = yes } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				AND = {
					OR = {
						trait = cynical
						trait = zealous
						trait = sympathy_old_world_group
						trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_cadai_group
						trait = sympathy_cytharai_group
						trait = sympathy_dwarf_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_halfling_gods_group
						trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
					}
					trait = erudite
					OR = { is_strong_trigger = yes is_weak_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
			change_learning = 2
			remove_trait = idolizer
		}

		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } educator = { trait = kind } prisoner = no }
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.122 } } # Let educator decide on intervention
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = adolescence.121 } # Go direct to trait change
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.121 Idolizer: the change
# The idolizer trait matures into the picked trait.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.121
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence121a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_zealous } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence121b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_erudite } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence121c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_weak } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence121
		trigger = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_zealous }
		remove_trait = idolizer
		add_trait = zealous
		clr_character_flag = idolizer_to_zealous
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence121
		trigger = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_erudite }
		remove_trait = idolizer
		add_trait = erudite
		clr_character_flag = idolizer_to_erudite
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence121
		trigger = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_weak }
		remove_trait = idolizer
		add_trait = feeble
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				70 = { modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = content } }
				30 = { character_event = { id = physique.120 days = 900 random = 400 } } #Change physical state event
			}
		}
		lose_weight_small_effect = yes
		clr_character_flag = idolizer_to_weak
	}
}

# adolescence.122 Idolizer: the educator steps in
# A kind educator may make the child kind instead.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.122
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence122a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_zealous } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence122b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_erudite } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence122c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_weak } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence122 # The child will be like me
		FROM = { custom_tooltip = { text = idolizer_may_become_kind } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.124 } } }
		show_trait = kind
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence122 # Do not intervene
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = adolescence.121 } } } # Go to normal trait change
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_zealous } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = idolizer add_trait = zealous } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_erudite } }
			tooltip = { FROM = { remove_trait = idolizer add_trait = erudite } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_weak } }
			tooltip = {
				FROM = { remove_trait = idolizer add_trait = feeble lose_weight_small_effect = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.124 Idolizer: the educator's verdict
# Tells the child what the educator's guidance came to.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent, FROM the educator.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.124
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence124a
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_zealous } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence124b
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_erudite } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCadolescence124c
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_weak } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence124
		remove_trait = idolizer
		add_trait = kind
		clr_character_flag = idolizer_to_zealous
		clr_character_flag = idolizer_to_erudite
		clr_character_flag = idolizer_to_weak
		if = {
			limit = { educator = { ai = no } }
			educator = { set_character_flag = achievement_follow_me }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence14
		trigger = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_zealous }
		remove_trait = idolizer
		add_trait = zealous
		clr_character_flag = idolizer_to_zealous
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence14
		trigger = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_erudite }
		remove_trait = idolizer
		add_trait = erudite
		clr_character_flag = idolizer_to_erudite
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDadolescence14
		trigger = { has_character_flag = idolizer_to_weak }
		remove_trait = idolizer
		add_trait = feeble
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				70 = { modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = content } }
				30 = { character_event = { id = physique.120 days = 900 random = 400 } } #Change physical state event
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = idolizer_to_weak
	}
}

# adolescence.3 First love
# Hidden. At 14, may start a romance at court (adolescence.200) or a dream
# about an older courtier (adolescence.250).
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.3

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		age = 14
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		is_ill = no
		NOT = { has_character_flag = is_child_of_destiny }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			70 = { # No events
			}
			15 = {
				character_event = { id = adolescence.200 days = 30 random = 120 } # Romance at court
			}
			15 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOT = {
						educator = {
							any_courtier = {
								age = 16
								NOR = {
									age = 35
									same_sex = ROOT
									trait = incapable
									trait = ugly
									trait = blinded
									trait = celibate
									trait = eunuch
									has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
									has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = adolescence.250 days = 30 random = 120 } # Romantic dreams of older courtier...
			}
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.200 Romance at court
# The adolescent pines for someone at court and may try to meet them.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from adolescence.3.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.200
	desc = EVTDESCadolescence200
	picture = GFX_evt_noble_in_castle
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = { }

	option = { # Farrier's apprentice
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence200
		trigger = { is_female = yes }
		ai_chance = { factor = 46 }
		set_character_flag = adolescence200_farrier
		character_event = { id = adolescence.201 days = 30 }
	}

	option = { # Carpenter's daughter male
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence200_male
		trigger = { is_female = no }
		ai_chance = { factor = 46 }
		set_character_flag = adolescence200_carpenter_male
		character_event = { id = adolescence.201 days = 30 }
	}

	option = { # Carpenter's daughter
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence200
		trigger = { is_female = yes }
		ai_chance = { factor = 22 }
		add_trait = homosexual
		set_character_flag = adolescence200_carpenter
		character_event = { id = adolescence.201 days = 30 }
	}

	option = { # Farrier's apprentice Male
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence200_male
		trigger = { is_female = no }
		ai_chance = { factor = 22 }
		add_trait = homosexual
		set_character_flag = adolescence200_farrier_male
		character_event = { id = adolescence.201 days = 30 }
	}

	option = { # I don't have time for this
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence200
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
}

# adolescence.250 A strange dream
# The adolescent wakes from a dream about an older courtier.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from adolescence.3.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.250
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	desc = EVTDESCadolescence250a

	is_triggered_only = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		educator = {
			any_courtier = {
				age = 16
				NOR = {
					character = ROOT
					age = 35
					same_sex = ROOT
					trait = incapable
					trait = ugly
					trait = blinded
					trait = celibate
					trait = eunuch
					has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
					has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
				}
			}
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		educator = {
			random_courtier = {
				limit = {
					age = 16
					NOR = {
						character = ROOT
						age = 35
						same_sex = ROOT
						trait = incapable
						trait = ugly
						trait = blinded
						trait = celibate
						trait = eunuch
						has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
						has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = lustful_target
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence250 # Homosexual version
		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = adolescence.251 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence250 # Lustful/Chaste version
		ai_chance = { factor = 78 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = adolescence.252 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDadolescence250 # Don't know don't care
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# adolescence.251 Thinking of them
# The adolescent cannot stop thinking about the courtier, which may lead to
# lust or chastity.
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.251
	picture = GFX_evt_stone_church
	desc = EVTDESCadolescence251a

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence251 # Homosexual
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = homosexual } } add_trait = homosexual }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence251 # Homosexual + chaste
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = homosexual } } add_trait = homosexual }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = chaste } } add_trait = chaste }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence251 # Homosexual + lustful
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = homosexual } } add_trait = homosexual }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = lustful } } add_trait = lustful }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDadolescence251 # I'm corrupted
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
		prestige = -50
	}
}

# adolescence.205 The educator's influence
# Hidden. An educator with a high skill may try to coach the child in it
# (adolescence.210-215).
# Scope: ROOT is the adolescent. Fired from on_adolescence_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.205
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOR = {
			trait = incapable
			is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
			has_character_modifier = the_dungeon
			has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
		}
		age = 14
		NOT = { age = 16 }
		educator = { always = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = { #If we have more than 2 high skills
			limit = {
				educator = {
					OR = {
						AND = { diplomacy = 12 martial = 12 }
						AND = { diplomacy = 12 stewardship = 12 }
						AND = { diplomacy = 12 intrigue = 12 }
						AND = { diplomacy = 12 learning = 12 }
						AND = { martial = 12 stewardship = 12 }
						AND = { martial = 12 intrigue = 12 }
						AND = { martial = 12 learning = 12 }
						AND = { stewardship = 12 intrigue = 12 }
						AND = { stewardship = 12 learning = 12 }
						AND = { intrigue = 12 learning = 12 }
					}
					OR = {
						AND = { ROOT = { NOT = { trait = ambitious } } NOT = { is_rival = ROOT } }
						AND = { ROOT = { NOT = { trait = diligent } } NOT = { trait = stressed } }
						AND = {
							ROOT = { NOT = { trait = patient } }
							NOT = { has_character_modifier = out_of_patience }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.215 days = 30 random = 20 } }
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						educator = {
							NOT = {
								diplomacy = 12
								OR = {
									AND = {
										ROOT = { NOT = { trait = honest } }
										NOT = { trait = trusting }
									}
									AND = {
										ROOT = { NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
										NOT = { has_character_modifier = chattermouth }
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.210 days = 30 random = 20 } }
				}
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						educator = {
							NOT = {
								martial = 12
								OR = {
									AND = {
										ROOT = { NOT = { trait = brave } }
										NOT = { trait = stressed }
									}
									AND = {
										ROOT = { NOT = { trait = wroth } }
										NOT = { trait = cruel }
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.211 days = 30 random = 20 } }
				}
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						educator = {
							NOT = {
								stewardship = 12
								OR = {
									AND = {
										ROOT = { NOT = { trait = just } }
										NOT = { holds_favor_on = ROOT }
									}
									ROOT = { NOT = { trait = temperate } }
								}
							}
						}
					}
					educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.212 days = 30 random = 20 } }
				}
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						educator = {
							NOT = {
								intrigue = 12
								OR = {
									AND = {
										ROOT = { NOT = { trait = deceitful } }
										NOT = { trait = paranoid }
									}
									AND = {
										ROOT = { NOT = { trait = cynical } }
										NOT = { has_character_modifier = impious_teachings }
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.213 days = 30 random = 20 } }
				}
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						educator = {
							NOT = {
								learning = 12
								OR = {
									AND = {
										ROOT = { NOT = { trait = erudite } }
										NOT = { trait = stubborn }
									}
									ROOT = { NOT = { trait = chaste } }
								}
							}
						}
					}
					educator = { character_event = { id = adolescence.214 days = 30 random = 20 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# adolescence.210 Coaching in diplomacy
# The educator offers the ward extra lessons in diplomacy.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the ward.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.210
	desc = EVTDESCadolescence210
	picture = GFX_evt_monk
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		diplomacy = 12
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		OR = {
			AND = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = honest } } NOT = { trait = trusting } }
			AND = {
				FROM = { NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = chattermouth }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = { factor = 30 current_heir = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 15
				NOT = { current_heir = { character = FROM } }
				any_child = { character = FROM }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				NOR = { current_heir = { character = FROM } any_child = { character = FROM } }
				OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = diligent }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = trusting }
		}
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence210
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = honest } } NOT = { trait = trusting } }
		FROM = {
			if = { limit = { trait = deceitful } remove_trait = deceitful }
			add_trait = honest
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = too_honest years = 5 }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = deceitful }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_hypocrite years = 10 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = { factor = 15 current_heir = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 30
				NOT = { current_heir = { character = FROM } }
				any_child = { character = FROM }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 10 OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = diligent } }
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = gregarious }
		}
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence210
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = chattermouth }
		}
		FROM = { if = { limit = { trait = shy } remove_trait = shy } add_trait = gregarious }
		add_character_modifier = { name = chattermouth years = 5 }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = shy }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_hypocrite years = 10 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence210
	}
}

# adolescence.211 Coaching in martial
# The educator offers the ward extra lessons in martial.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the ward.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.211
	desc = EVTDESCadolescence211
	picture = GFX_evt_melee
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		martial = 12
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		OR = {
			AND = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = brave } } NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			AND = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = wroth } } NOT = { trait = cruel } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = { factor = 30 current_heir = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 15
				NOT = { current_heir = { character = FROM } }
				any_child = { character = FROM }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				NOR = { current_heir = { character = FROM } any_child = { character = FROM } }
				OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = diligent }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = brave }
		}
		tooltip_info = martial
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence211
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = brave } } NOT = { trait = stressed } }
		FROM = { if = { limit = { trait = craven } remove_trait = craven } add_trait = brave }
		add_trait = stressed
		if = {
			limit = { trait = craven }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_hypocrite years = 10 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 20 trait = wroth } }
		tooltip_info = martial
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence211
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = wroth } } NOT = { trait = cruel } }
		FROM = { if = { limit = { trait = patient } remove_trait = patient } add_trait = wroth }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = patient }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_hypocrite years = 10 }
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = kind } remove_trait = kind }
		else = { add_trait = cruel }
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence210
	}
}

# adolescence.212 Coaching in stewardship
# The educator offers the ward extra lessons in stewardship.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the ward.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.212
	desc = EVTDESCadolescence212
	picture = GFX_evt_relaxed_ruler
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		stewardship = 12
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		OR = {
			AND = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = just } } NOT = { holds_favor_on = FROM } }
			FROM = { NOT = { trait = temperate } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = { factor = 30 current_heir = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 15
				NOT = { current_heir = { character = FROM } }
				any_child = { character = FROM }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				NOR = { current_heir = { character = FROM } any_child = { character = FROM } }
				OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = diligent }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = just }
		}
		tooltip_info = stewardship
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence212
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = just } } NOT = { holds_favor_on = FROM } }
		FROM = { if = { limit = { trait = arbitrary } remove_trait = arbitrary } add_trait = just }
		reverse_add_favor = FROM
		if = {
			limit = { trait = arbitrary }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_hypocrite years = 10 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = { factor = 15 current_heir = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 30
				NOT = { current_heir = { character = FROM } }
				any_child = { character = FROM }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				NOR = { current_heir = { character = FROM } any_child = { character = FROM } }
				OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = diligent }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = temperate }
		}
		tooltip_info = stewardship
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence212
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = temperate } } }
		FROM = {
			if = {
				limit = { trait = gluttonous }
				remove_trait = gluttonous
				lose_weight_small_effect = yes
				weight_trait_check_effect = yes
			}
			add_trait = temperate
			lose_weight_small_effect = yes
			weight_trait_check_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = gluttonous }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_hypocrite years = 10 }
		}
		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_boring years = 10 }
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence210
	}
}

# adolescence.213 Coaching in intrigue
# The educator offers the ward extra lessons in intrigue.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the ward.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.213
	desc = EVTDESCadolescence213
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		intrigue = 12
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		OR = {
			AND = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = deceitful } } NOT = { trait = paranoid } }
			AND = {
				FROM = { NOT = { trait = cynical } }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = impious_teachings }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = { factor = 30 current_heir = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 15
				NOT = { current_heir = { character = FROM } }
				any_child = { character = FROM }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				NOR = { current_heir = { character = FROM } any_child = { character = FROM } }
				OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = diligent }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = deceitful }
		}
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence213
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = deceitful } } NOT = { trait = paranoid } }
		FROM = { if = { limit = { trait = honest } remove_trait = honest } add_trait = deceitful }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = honest }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_hypocrite years = 10 }
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = trusting } remove_trait = trusting }
		else = { add_trait = paranoid }
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = { factor = 15 current_heir = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 30
				NOT = { current_heir = { character = FROM } }
				any_child = { character = FROM }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				NOR = { current_heir = { character = FROM } any_child = { character = FROM } }
				OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = diligent }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = cynical }
		}
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence213
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { trait = cynical } }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = impious_teachings }
		}
		FROM = { if = { limit = { trait = zealous } remove_trait = zealous } add_trait = cynical }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = zealous }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_hypocrite years = 10 }
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = impious_teachings years = 5 }
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence210
	}
}

# adolescence.215 Coaching in several skills
# The educator offers lessons in more than one skill.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the ward.
character_event = {
	id = adolescence.215
	desc = EVTDESCadolescence215
	picture = GFX_evt_noble_in_castle
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes }
		OR = {
			AND = { diplomacy = 12 martial = 12 }
			AND = { diplomacy = 12 stewardship = 12 }
			AND = { diplomacy = 12 intrigue = 12 }
			AND = { diplomacy = 12 learning = 12 }
			AND = { martial = 12 stewardship = 12 }
			AND = { martial = 12 intrigue = 12 }
			AND = { martial = 12 learning = 12 }
			AND = { stewardship = 12 intrigue = 12 }
			AND = { stewardship = 12 learning = 12 }
			AND = { intrigue = 12 learning = 12 }
		}
		OR = {
			AND = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = ambitious } } NOT = { is_rival = FROM } }
			AND = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = diligent } } NOT = { trait = stressed } }
			AND = {
				FROM = { NOT = { trait = patient } }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = out_of_patience }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # Give ambitious
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = { factor = 30 current_heir = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 15
				NOT = { current_heir = { character = FROM } }
				any_child = { character = FROM }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				NOR = { current_heir = { character = FROM } any_child = { character = FROM } }
				OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = diligent }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = ambitious }
		}
		name = EVTOPTAadolescence215
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = ambitious } } NOT = { is_rival = FROM } }
		FROM = {
			if = { limit = { trait = content } remove_trait = content }
			add_trait = ambitious
			add_rival = ROOT
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = content }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_hypocrite years = 10 }
		}
	}

	option = { # Give diligent
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = { factor = 20 current_heir = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 20
				NOT = { current_heir = { character = FROM } }
				any_child = { character = FROM }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				NOR = { current_heir = { character = FROM } any_child = { character = FROM } }
				OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = diligent }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = diligent }
		}
		name = EVTOPTBadolescence215
		trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = diligent } } NOT = { trait = stressed } }
		FROM = {
			if = { limit = { trait = slothful } remove_trait = slothful }
			add_trait = diligent
		}
		add_trait = stressed
		if = {
			limit = { trait = slothful }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_hypocrite years = 10 }
		}
	}

	option = { # Give patient
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = { factor = 15 current_heir = { character = FROM } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 30
				NOT = { current_heir = { character = FROM } }
				any_child = { character = FROM }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				NOR = { current_heir = { character = FROM } any_child = { character = FROM } }
				OR = { trait = kind trait = charitable trait = diligent }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 5 trait = patient }
		}
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence215
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { trait = patient } }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = out_of_patience }
		}
		FROM = { if = { limit = { trait = wroth } remove_trait = wroth } add_trait = patient }
		add_character_modifier = { name = out_of_patience years = 5 }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = wroth }
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_hypocrite years = 10 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
		name = EVTOPTCadolescence210
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 3: education traits
#
# Namespace: education (was ZE.14000-14999 in ze_education_trait_events.txt).
#
#   education.100-510   one block per focus: +0 the outcome, +10 the educator
#   education.600       the prison flag
#   education.700-710   no focus
###############################################################################

namespace = education

# education.100 Education in diplomacy
# The adolescent finishes their diplomacy education and gets an education
# trait of level 1-4, set by their skill, their educator, the prison and
# their traits.
# Scope: ROOT is the new adult. Fired from on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = education.100
	picture = GFX_evt_council_speaker
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation100a
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation100b
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation100c
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation100d
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4 }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_focus = focus_ed_diplomacy
		NOT = { has_character_flag = is_child_of_destiny }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = is_being_graded
		educator = { save_event_target_as = education_outcome_educator }
		clear_focus = yes
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { is_smart_trigger = yes trait = diligent } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = slothful }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_fussy trait = fussy }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 OR = { has_character_flag = was_fussy trait = fussy } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_affectionate trait = affectionate }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_curious trait = curious }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_affectionate trait = affectionate }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_curious trait = curious }
				}
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2
			}
			15 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_fussy trait = fussy }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.7
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 OR = { has_character_flag = was_fussy trait = fussy } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_affectionate trait = affectionate }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_curious trait = curious }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_affectionate trait = affectionate }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_curious trait = curious } }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.4 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_fussy trait = fussy }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { has_character_flag = was_fussy trait = fussy } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_affectionate trait = affectionate }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_curious trait = curious }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_affectionate trait = affectionate }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_curious trait = curious } }
				modifier = { factor = 20 has_character_flag = is_child_of_destiny }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned
		clr_character_flag = was_willful
		clr_character_flag = was_brooding
		clr_character_flag = was_indolent
		clr_character_flag = was_playful
		clr_character_flag = was_conscientious
		clr_character_flag = was_fussy
		clr_character_flag = was_curious
		clr_character_flag = was_idolizer
		clr_character_flag = was_haughty
		clr_character_flag = was_affectionate
		clr_character_flag = was_timid
		clr_character_flag = was_rowdy
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation100
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1 }
		add_trait = naive_appeaser
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBeducation100
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2 }
		add_trait = underhanded_rogue
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_ogre }
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_martial = 1 }
				50 = { }
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCeducation100
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3 }
		add_trait = charismatic_negotiator
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_ogre }
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_martial = 1 }
				50 = { }
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDeducation100
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4 }
		add_trait = grey_eminence
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_ogre }
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_martial = 2 }
				50 = { }
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = is_being_graded
		hidden_tooltip = {
			# Notify educator
			event_target:education_outcome_educator = { character_event = { id = education.110 } }
			if = {
				limit = { # Notify mother if primary heir
					mother = {
						event_target:education_outcome_educator = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
						ROOT = { is_primary_heir = PREV }
					}
				}
				mother = { character_event = { id = education.110 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { # Notify father if primary heir
					father = {
						event_target:education_outcome_educator = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
						ROOT = { is_primary_heir = PREV }
					}
				}
				father = { character_event = { id = education.110 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

		name = EVTOPTBeducation110
		trigger = { FROM = { trait = underhanded_rogue } }
		tooltip = { FROM = { add_trait = underhanded_rogue } }

		tooltip = { FROM = { add_trait = charismatic_negotiator } }
