# education.200 Education in martial
# The adolescent finishes their martial education and gets an education
# trait of level 1-4, set by their skill, their educator, the prison and
# their traits.
# Scope: ROOT is the new adult. Fired from on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = education.200
	picture = GFX_evt_war_planning
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation200a
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation200b
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation200c
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation200d
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4 }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_focus = focus_ed_martial
		NOT = { has_character_flag = is_child_of_destiny }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = is_being_graded
		educator = { save_event_target_as = education_outcome_educator }
		clear_focus = yes
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { is_smart_trigger = yes trait = diligent } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = slothful }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_rowdy trait = rowdy }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { has_character_flag = was_rowdy trait = rowdy } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful }
				}
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2
			}
			15 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.7
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_rowdy trait = rowdy }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_rowdy trait = rowdy } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful } }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.4 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_rowdy trait = rowdy }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_rowdy trait = rowdy } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_willful trait = willful } }
				modifier = { factor = 20 has_character_flag = is_child_of_destiny }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned
		clr_character_flag = was_willful
		clr_character_flag = was_brooding
		clr_character_flag = was_indolent
		clr_character_flag = was_playful
		clr_character_flag = was_conscientious
		clr_character_flag = was_fussy
		clr_character_flag = was_curious
		clr_character_flag = was_idolizer
		clr_character_flag = was_haughty
		clr_character_flag = was_affectionate
		clr_character_flag = was_timid
		clr_character_flag = was_rowdy
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation200
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1 }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_ogre }
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_martial = 1 }
				50 = { }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					culture_group = norscan_group
					culture_group = kurgan_group
					culture_group = norscan_group
					culture_group = hung_group
					culture_group = beastman_group
					liege = { is_tribal = yes }
				}
			}
			add_trait = barbarian_1
		}
		else = { add_trait = misguided_warrior }
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBeducation200
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2 }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_ogre }
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_martial = 3 }
				50 = { }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					culture_group = norscan_group
					culture_group = kurgan_group
					culture_group = norscan_group
					culture_group = hung_group
					culture_group = beastman_group
					liege = { is_tribal = yes }
				}
			}
			add_trait = barbarian_2
		}
		else = { add_trait = tough_soldier }
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCeducation200
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					culture_group = norscan_group
					culture_group = kurgan_group
					culture_group = norscan_group
					culture_group = hung_group
					culture_group = beastman_group
					liege = { is_tribal = yes }
				}
			}
			add_trait = barbarian_3
		}
		else = { add_trait = skilled_tactician }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_ogre }
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_martial = 2 }
				50 = { }
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDeducation200
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4 }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_ogre }
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_martial = 2 }
				50 = { }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					culture_group = norscan_group
					culture_group = kurgan_group
					culture_group = norscan_group
					culture_group = hung_group
					culture_group = beastman_group
					liege = { is_tribal = yes }
				}
			}
			add_trait = barbarian_4
		}
		else = { add_trait = brilliant_strategist }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				10 = { add_trait = light_foot_leader }
				10 = { add_trait = heavy_infantry_leader }
				10 = { add_trait = cavalry_leader }
				10 = { add_trait = inspiring_leader }
				10 = { add_trait = trickster }
				10 = { add_trait = organizer }
				10 = { add_trait = defensive_leader }
				10 = { add_trait = experimenter }
				10 = { add_trait = flanker }
				10 = { add_trait = aggressive_leader }
				10 = { add_trait = siege_leader }
				30 = {
					trigger = {
						OR = {
							culture = cathayan
							has_character_flag = taught_cathayan_strategy
							has_character_flag = originated_from_cathayan_court
						}
					}
					add_trait = master_of_flame
				}
				30 = {
					trigger = {
						OR = {
							culture = cathayan
							has_character_flag = taught_cathayan_strategy
							has_character_flag = originated_from_cathayan_court
						}
					}
					add_trait = logistics_expert
				}
				30 = {
					trigger = {
						OR = {
							culture = cathayan
							has_character_flag = taught_cathayan_strategy
							has_character_flag = originated_from_cathayan_court
						}
					}
					add_trait = levy_coordinator
				}
				30 = {
					trigger = {
						OR = {
							culture = cathayan
							has_character_flag = taught_cathayan_strategy
							has_character_flag = originated_from_cathayan_court
						}
					}
					add_trait = sapper
				}
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = is_being_graded
		hidden_tooltip = {
			# Notify educator
			event_target:education_outcome_educator = { character_event = { id = education.210 } }
			if = {
				limit = { # Notify mother if primary heir
					mother = {
						event_target:education_outcome_educator = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
						ROOT = { is_primary_heir = PREV }
					}
				}
				mother = { character_event = { id = education.210 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { # Notify father if primary heir
					father = {
						event_target:education_outcome_educator = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
						ROOT = { is_primary_heir = PREV }
					}
				}
				father = { character_event = { id = education.210 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# education.300 Education in stewardship
# The adolescent finishes their stewardship education and gets an education
# trait of level 1-4, set by their skill, their educator, the prison and
# their traits.
# Scope: ROOT is the new adult. Fired from on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = education.300
	picture = GFX_evt_relaxed_ruler
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_economy

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation300a
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation300b
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation300c
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation300d
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4 }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_focus = focus_ed_stewardship
		NOT = { has_character_flag = is_child_of_destiny }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = is_being_graded
		educator = { save_event_target_as = education_outcome_educator }
		clear_focus = yes
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { is_smart_trigger = yes trait = diligent } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = slothful }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_indolent trait = indolent }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_haughty trait = haughty }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_indolent trait = indolent }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_haughty trait = haughty }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_conscientious trait = conscientious }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_conscientious trait = conscientious }
				}
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2
			}
			15 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_indolent trait = indolent }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_haughty trait = haughty }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.7
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_indolent trait = indolent }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.7
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_haughty trait = haughty }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_conscientious trait = conscientious }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_conscientious trait = conscientious }
				}
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.4 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				modifier = {

					AND = {

					}
				}

					OR = { has_character_flag = was_indolent trait = indolent }

					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_conscientious trait = conscientious }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
				}
				modifier = {

					OR = { has_character_flag = was_conscientious trait = conscientious }

				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_rowdy trait = rowdy }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_affectionate trait = affectionate }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 OR = { has_character_flag = was_rowdy trait = rowdy } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.7
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_affectionate trait = affectionate }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_playful trait = playful }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_fussy trait = fussy }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_playful trait = playful } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_fussy trait = fussy } }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.4 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_rowdy trait = rowdy }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_affectionate trait = affectionate }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { has_character_flag = was_rowdy trait = rowdy } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_affectionate trait = affectionate }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_playful trait = playful }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_fussy trait = fussy }
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_playful trait = playful } }
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_fussy trait = fussy } }
				modifier = { factor = 20 has_character_flag = is_child_of_destiny }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned
		clr_character_flag = was_willful
		clr_character_flag = was_brooding
		clr_character_flag = was_indolent
		clr_character_flag = was_playful
		clr_character_flag = was_conscientious
		clr_character_flag = was_fussy
		clr_character_flag = was_curious
		clr_character_flag = was_idolizer
		clr_character_flag = was_haughty
		clr_character_flag = was_affectionate
		clr_character_flag = was_timid
		clr_character_flag = was_rowdy
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation400
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1 }
		add_trait = amateurish_plotter
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBeducation400
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2 }
		add_trait = flamboyant_schemer
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCeducation400
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3 }
		add_trait = intricate_webweaver
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDeducation400
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4 }
		add_trait = elusive_shadow
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = is_being_graded
		hidden_tooltip = {
			# Notify educator
			event_target:education_outcome_educator = { character_event = { id = education.410 } }
			if = {
				limit = { # Notify mother if primary heir
					mother = {
						event_target:education_outcome_educator = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
						ROOT = { is_primary_heir = PREV }
					}
				}
				mother = { character_event = { id = education.410 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { # Notify father if primary heir
					father = {
						event_target:education_outcome_educator = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
						ROOT = { is_primary_heir = PREV }
					}
				}
				father = { character_event = { id = education.410 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# education.500 Education in learning
# The adolescent finishes their learning education and gets an education
# trait of level 1-4, set by their skill, their educator, the prison and
# their traits.
# Scope: ROOT is the new adult. Fired from on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = education.500
	picture = GFX_evt_book_carolingian_script
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation500a
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation500b
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation500c
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3 }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeducation500d
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4 }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_focus = focus_ed_learning
		NOT = { has_character_flag = is_child_of_destiny }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = is_being_graded
		educator = { save_event_target_as = education_outcome_educator }
		clear_focus = yes
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { is_smart_trigger = yes trait = diligent } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = slothful }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_playful trait = playful }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_playful trait = playful }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid } }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2
			}
			15 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_playful trait = playful }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.7
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.7
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_playful trait = playful }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid } }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 is_smart_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = diligent }
				modifier = { factor = 0.4 trait = slothful }
				modifier = { factor = 0 OR = { trait = incapable trait = imbecile trait = inbred } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_playful trait = playful }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_brooding trait = brooding }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_playful trait = playful }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					AND = {
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
						OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid }
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					OR = { has_character_flag = was_idolizer trait = idolizer }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { has_character_flag = was_timid trait = timid } }
				modifier = { factor = 20 has_character_flag = is_child_of_destiny }
				set_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned
		clr_character_flag = was_willful
		clr_character_flag = was_brooding
		clr_character_flag = was_indolent
		clr_character_flag = was_playful
		clr_character_flag = was_conscientious
		clr_character_flag = was_fussy
		clr_character_flag = was_curious
		clr_character_flag = was_idolizer
		clr_character_flag = was_haughty
		clr_character_flag = was_affectionate
		clr_character_flag = was_timid
		clr_character_flag = was_rowdy
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation500
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1 }
		add_trait = detached_priest
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_1
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBeducation500
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2 }
		add_trait = martial_cleric
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_ogre }
			random_list = {
				75 = { }
				25 = {
					if = {
						limit = { religion = great_maw }
						add_trait = lore_maw
						add_trait = ogre_slaughtermaster
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { religion = fire_mouth }
						add_trait = lore_fire
						add_trait = ogre_firemouth
					}
					random_lore_mage_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_halfling culture = gnome }
			random_list = {
				50 = { }
				25 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						OR = {
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_1
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_2
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_3
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_4
							trait = magic_potential_1
							trait = magic_potential_2
							trait = magic_potential_3
							trait = magic_potential_4
						}
					}
					add_trait = lore_shadows
					random_lore_mage_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_2
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTCeducation500
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3 }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_ogre }
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.2
						NOR = {
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_3
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_4
							trait = magic_potential_3
							trait = magic_potential_4
						}
					}
				}
				50 = {
					if = {
						limit = { religion = great_maw }
						add_trait = lore_maw
						add_trait = ogre_slaughtermaster
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { religion = fire_mouth }
						add_trait = lore_fire
						add_trait = ogre_firemouth
					}
					random_lore_mage_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_halfling culture = gnome }
			random_list = {
				40 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						NOR = {
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_3
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_4
							trait = magic_potential_3
							trait = magic_potential_4
						}
					}
				}
				25 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						OR = {
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_1
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_2
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_3
							has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_4
							trait = magic_potential_1
							trait = magic_potential_2
							trait = magic_potential_3
							trait = magic_potential_4
						}
					}
					add_trait = lore_shadows
					random_lore_mage_effect = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_trait = scholarly_theologian
		clr_character_flag = education_outcome_level_3

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTDeducation500
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_outcome_level_4 }

# education.700 Education without a focus
# The adolescent finishes their schooling with no education focus and picks
# up a weak education trait.
# Scope: ROOT is the new adult. Fired from on_adulthood.
character_event = {
	id = education.700
	picture = GFX_evt_noble_in_castle

	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = { text = EVTDESCeducation700a trigger = { has_character_flag = education_diplomacy } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeducation700b trigger = { has_character_flag = education_martial } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeducation700c trigger = { has_character_flag = education_stewardship } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeducation700d trigger = { has_character_flag = education_intrigue } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeducation700e trigger = { has_character_flag = education_learning } }

	trigger = { # Make sure we have no education focus and no education outcome already set
		NOR = {
			has_character_flag = is_being_graded
			has_focus = focus_ed_diplomacy
			has_focus = focus_ed_martial
			has_focus = focus_ed_stewardship
			has_focus = focus_ed_intrigue
			has_focus = focus_ed_learning
			trait = naive_appeaser
			trait = underhanded_rogue
			trait = charismatic_negotiator
			trait = grey_eminence
			trait = misguided_warrior
			trait = tough_soldier
			trait = skilled_tactician
			trait = brilliant_strategist
			trait = indulgent_wastrel
			trait = thrifty_clerk
			trait = fortune_builder
			trait = midas_touched
			trait = amateurish_plotter
			trait = flamboyant_schemer
			trait = intricate_webweaver
			trait = elusive_shadow
			trait = detached_priest
			trait = martial_cleric
			trait = scholarly_theologian
			trait = mastermind_theologian
			trait = barbarian_1
			trait = barbarian_2
			trait = barbarian_3
			trait = barbarian_4
			trait = mastermind_theologian
		}
		NOT = { has_character_flag = is_child_of_destiny }
	}

	immediate = {
		educator = { save_event_target_as = education_outcome_educator }
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_character_flag = education_diplomacy }
			10 = { set_character_flag = education_martial }
			10 = { set_character_flag = education_stewardship }
			10 = { set_character_flag = education_intrigue }
			10 = { set_character_flag = education_learning }
		}
		clr_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned
		clr_character_flag = was_willful
		clr_character_flag = was_brooding
		clr_character_flag = was_indolent
		clr_character_flag = was_playful
		clr_character_flag = was_conscientious
		clr_character_flag = was_fussy
		clr_character_flag = was_curious
		clr_character_flag = was_idolizer
		clr_character_flag = was_haughty
		clr_character_flag = was_affectionate
		clr_character_flag = was_timid
		clr_character_flag = was_rowdy
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation700
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_diplomacy }
		add_trait = naive_appeaser
		clr_character_flag = education_diplomacy
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation700
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_martial }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					culture_group = norscan_group
					culture_group = kurgan_group
					culture_group = norscan_group
					culture_group = hung_group
					culture_group = beastman_group
					liege = { is_tribal = yes }
				}
			}
			add_trait = barbarian_1
		}
		else = { add_trait = misguided_warrior }
		clr_character_flag = education_martial
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation700
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_stewardship }
		add_trait = indulgent_wastrel
		clr_character_flag = education_stewardship
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation700
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_intrigue }
		add_trait = amateurish_plotter
		clr_character_flag = education_intrigue
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation700
		trigger = { has_character_flag = education_learning }
		add_trait = detached_priest
		clr_character_flag = education_learning
	}

	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			# Notify educator
			event_target:education_outcome_educator = { character_event = { id = education.710 } }
			if = {
				limit = { # Notify mother if primary heir
					mother = {
						event_target:education_outcome_educator = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
						ROOT = { is_primary_heir = PREV }
					}
				}
				mother = { character_event = { id = education.710 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { # Notify father if primary heir
					father = {
						event_target:education_outcome_educator = { NOT = { character = PREV } }
						ROOT = { is_primary_heir = PREV }
					}
				}
				father = { character_event = { id = education.710 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# education.710 The educator hears: no focus
# The educator learns how the unfocused ward turned out.
# Scope: ROOT is the educator, FROM the new adult.
character_event = {
	id = education.710
	picture = GFX_evt_noble_in_castle

	desc = { text = EVTDESCeducation710a trigger = { FROM = { trait = naive_appeaser } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeducation710b trigger = { FROM = { trait = misguided_warrior } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeducation710b trigger = { FROM = { trait = barbarian_1 } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeducation710c trigger = { FROM = { trait = indulgent_wastrel } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeducation710d trigger = { FROM = { trait = amateurish_plotter } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeducation710e trigger = { FROM = { trait = detached_priest } } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation710
		trigger = { FROM = { trait = naive_appeaser } }
		tooltip = { FROM = { add_trait = naive_appeaser } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation710
		trigger = { FROM = { trait = misguided_warrior } }
		tooltip = { FROM = { add_trait = misguided_warrior } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation710
		trigger = { FROM = { trait = indulgent_wastrel } }
		tooltip = { FROM = { add_trait = indulgent_wastrel } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation710
		trigger = { FROM = { trait = amateurish_plotter } }
		tooltip = { FROM = { add_trait = amateurish_plotter } }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAeducation710
		trigger = { FROM = { trait = detached_priest } }
		tooltip = { FROM = { add_trait = detached_priest } }
	}
}

# education.600 Schooled in a cell
# Hidden. A child of 12 or more thrown in prison gets a flag that lowers
# their education outcome.
# Scope: ROOT is the child. Fired from on_become_imprisoned_any_reason.
character_event = {
	id = education.600

	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_adult = no
		age = 12
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = education_effect_imprisoned
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 4: introducing the heir
#
# Namespace: heir (was ZE.22899-22909 in ze_introduce_heir.txt).
#
#   heir.1              the vignette tombola
#   heir.10-11          start and end
#   heir.12-19          vignettes
###############################################################################

namespace = heir

# heir.1 The introduction tombola
# Hidden. Picks the next vignette of the heir's introduction (heir.12-19),
# at most four, then ends it (heir.11).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler; event target introduced_heir.
character_event = {
	id = heir.1
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = { clr_flags_introduce_heir_effect = yes }

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { #If you had at least 4 events already
				limit = {
					calc_true_if = {
						amount = 4
						has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_1
						has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_2
						has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_2B
						has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_3
						has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_3B
						has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_4
						has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_5
						has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_6
						has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_7
						has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_8
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = heir.11 days = 10 random = 10 } #Heir is groomed!
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					100 = { #Vassal offers to teach heir the scripture for a favor
						random = {
							chance = 75
							character_event = { id = heir.12 days = 10 random = 5 } #send event
							set_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_1
							break = yes
						}

						trigger = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_1 }
							any_vassal = {
								ai = yes
								religion = ROOT
								prisoner = no
								piety = 0
								NOT = { holds_favor_on = ROOT }
								NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
								OR = { trait = theologian trait = scholar learning = 12 }
							}
						}
					}
					100 = { #Heir impresses visiting vassal
						random = {
							chance = 75
							character_event = { id = heir.13 days = 21 random = 5 } # send event
							set_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_2
							break = yes
						}

						trigger = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_2 }
							any_vassal = {
								ai = yes
								prisoner = no
								NOR = {
									trait = incapable
									is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
									has_opinion_modifier = {
										modifier = insulted
										who = event_target:introduced_heir
									}
									has_opinion_modifier = {
										modifier = opinion_impressed
										who = event_target:introduced_heir
									}
								}
							}
							event_target:introduced_heir = {
								OR = {
									trait = affectionate
									has_character_flag = was_affectionate
									trait = playful
									has_character_flag = was_playful
									trait = curious
									has_character_flag = was_curious
									trait = conscientious
									has_character_flag = was_conscientious
									trait = idolizer
									has_character_flag = was_idolizer
								}
							}
						}
					}
					100 = { #Heir impresses visiting vassal (again!)
						random = {
							chance = 75
							character_event = { id = heir.13 days = 21 random = 5 } # send event
							set_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_2_B
							break = yes
						}

						trigger = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_2_B }
							any_vassal = {
								ai = yes
								prisoner = no
								NOR = {
									trait = incapable
									is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
									has_opinion_modifier = {
										modifier = insulted
										who = event_target:introduced_heir
									}
									has_opinion_modifier = {
										modifier = opinion_impressed
										who = event_target:introduced_heir
									}
								}
							}
							event_target:introduced_heir = {
								OR = {
									trait = affectionate
									has_character_flag = was_affectionate
									trait = playful
									has_character_flag = was_playful
									trait = curious
									has_character_flag = was_curious
									trait = conscientious
									has_character_flag = was_conscientious
									trait = idolizer
									has_character_flag = was_idolizer
								}
							}
						}
					}
					100 = { #Heir accidently insults visiting vassal
						random = {
							chance = 75
							character_event = { id = heir.14 days = 15 random = 5 } # send event
							set_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_3
							break = yes
						}

						trigger = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_3 }
							any_vassal = {
								ai = yes
								prisoner = no
								NOR = {
									trait = incapable
									is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
									has_opinion_modifier = {
										modifier = insulted
										who = event_target:introduced_heir
									}
									has_opinion_modifier = {
										modifier = opinion_impressed
										who = event_target:introduced_heir
									}
								}
							}
							event_target:introduced_heir = {
								OR = {
									trait = haughty
									has_character_flag = was_haughty
									trait = rowdy
									has_character_flag = was_rowdy
									trait = willful
									has_character_flag = was_willful
									trait = indolent
									has_character_flag = was_indolent
									trait = fussy
									has_character_flag = was_fussy
								}
							}
						}
					}
					100 = { #Heir accidently insults visiting vassal (again!)
						random = {
							chance = 75
							character_event = { id = heir.14 days = 15 random = 5 } # send event
							set_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_3_B
							break = yes
						}

						trigger = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_3_B }
							any_vassal = {
								ai = yes
								prisoner = no
								NOR = {
									trait = incapable
									is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
									has_opinion_modifier = {
										modifier = insulted
										who = event_target:introduced_heir
									}
									has_opinion_modifier = {
										modifier = opinion_impressed
										who = event_target:introduced_heir
									}
								}
							}
							event_target:introduced_heir = {
								OR = {
									trait = haughty
									has_character_flag = was_haughty
									trait = rowdy
									has_character_flag = was_rowdy
									trait = willful
									has_character_flag = was_willful
									trait = indolent
									has_character_flag = was_indolent
									trait = fussy
									has_character_flag = was_fussy
								}
							}
						}
					}

					50 = { #Heir befriends vassal child
						random = {
							chance = 75
							character_event = { id = heir.15 days = 32 random = 5 } # send event
							set_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_4
							break = yes
						}

						trigger = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_4 }
							event_target:introduced_heir = {
								NOR = { trait = timid has_character_flag = was_timid }
							}
							any_vassal = {
								ai = yes
								prisoner = no
								NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
								any_child = {
									ai = yes
									age = 8
									is_adult = no
									prisoner = no
									is_landed = no
									NOR = {
										is_friend = event_target:introduced_heir
										is_rival = event_target:introduced_heir
										sibling = event_target:introduced_heir
										trait = incapable
										is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					50 = { #Heir rivals vassal child
						random = {
							chance = 75
							character_event = { id = heir.16 days = 13 random = 5 }
							set_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_5
							break = yes
						}
						trigger = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_5 }
							event_target:introduced_heir = {
								NOR = { trait = affectionate has_character_flag = was_affectionate }
							}
							any_vassal = {
								ai = yes
								prisoner = no
								NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
								any_child = {
									ai = yes
									age = 8
									is_adult = no
									prisoner = no
									is_landed = no
									NOR = {
										is_friend = event_target:introduced_heir
										is_rival = event_target:introduced_heir
										sibling = event_target:introduced_heir
										trait = incapable
										is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					10 = { #Heir underperforms and becomes depressed
						random = {
							chance = 10
							character_event = { id = heir.17 days = 14 random = 5 } #send event
							set_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_6
							break = yes
						}
						trigger = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_6 }
							event_target:introduced_heir = {
								NOR = {
									trait = depressed
									trait = indolent
									has_character_flag = was_indolent
								}
								OR = {
									trait = brooding
									has_character_flag = was_brooding
									trait = conscientious
									has_character_flag = was_conscientious
									trait = fussy
									has_character_flag = was_fussy
									trait = stressed
								}
							}
						}
					}
					20 = { #Vassal offers to teach heir to fight for a favor
						random = {
							chance = 75
							character_event = { id = heir.18 days = 14 random = 5 } #send event
							set_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_7
							break = yes
						}
						trigger = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_7 }
							any_vassal = {
								ai = yes
								prisoner = no
								NOR = {
									holds_favor_on = ROOT
									trait = incapable
									is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
								}
								OR = { trait = strategist trait = duelist martial = 12 }
							}
						}
					}
					10 = { #Heir is infatuated with Vassal's spouse
						random = {
							chance = 50
							character_event = { id = heir.19 days = 10 random = 5 } #send event
							set_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_8
							break = yes
						}
						trigger = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = ZE_evt_heir_8 }
							event_target:introduced_heir = { is_female = no }
							any_vassal = {
								is_female = no
								ai = yes
								is_married = yes
								prisoner = no
								NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
								any_spouse = {
									is_female = yes
									ai = yes
									prisoner = no
									age = 20
									is_landed = no
									NOT = { age = 45 }
									NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				repeat_event = { id = heir.1 days = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# heir.10 The heir is introduced
# The ruler invites the vassals to meet the heir.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the heir. Fired from the introduce_heir
#   decision (decisions/ze_decisions.txt).
character_event = {
	id = heir.10
	desc = EVTDESCheir10
	picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAheir10

		event_target:introduced_heir = { prestige = 5 }
		character_event = { id = heir.1 } #send tombola
	}
}

# heir.11 The introduction ends
# The last vassal leaves and the heir is worn out but better known.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = heir.11
	desc = EVTDESCheir11
	picture = GFX_evt_son_asking_father

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes }
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes } } }
			clr_flags_introduce_heir_effect = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAheir11

		event_target:introduced_heir = {
			show_scope_change = no
			add_character_modifier = { modifier = groomed_to_perfection years = 20 }
		}

		clr_flags_introduce_heir_effect = yes
	}
}

# heir.12 A pious vassal offers lessons
# A learned vassal offers to teach the heir the faith's scripture in return
# for the ruler's favour.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = heir.12
	desc = EVTDESCheir12
	picture = GFX_evt_church_council

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes }
		any_vassal = {
			ai = yes
			religion = ROOT
			prisoner = no
			piety = 0
			NOR = { holds_favor_on = ROOT trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { trait = theologian trait = scholar learning = 12 }
		}
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes } } }
			clr_flags_introduce_heir_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		any_vassal = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				religion = ROOT
				prisoner = no
				piety = 0
				NOR = { holds_favor_on = ROOT trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				OR = { trait = theologian trait = scholar learning = 12 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = vassal_teacher
		}
	}

	#Accept offer
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAheir12

		event_target:introduced_heir = { change_learning = 1 piety = 50 }
		event_target:vassal_teacher = { add_favor = ROOT }
	}

	#Decline offer
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBheir12

		piety = -50

		event_target:vassal_teacher = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_disappointed who = ROOT years = 10 }
		}
	}
	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}
}

# heir.13 The heir impresses
# The heir charms a vassal with a well-turned compliment.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = heir.13
	desc = EVTDESCheir13
	picture = GFX_evt_dancing

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes }
		any_vassal = {
			ai = yes
			prisoner = no
			NOR = {
				trait = incapable
				is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
				has_opinion_modifier = { modifier = insulted who = event_target:introduced_heir }
				has_opinion_modifier = {
					modifier = opinion_impressed
					who = event_target:introduced_heir
				}
			}
		}
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes } } }
			clr_flags_introduce_heir_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		any_vassal = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				prisoner = no
				NOR = {
					trait = incapable
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						modifier = insulted
						who = event_target:introduced_heir
					}
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						modifier = opinion_impressed
						who = event_target:introduced_heir
					}
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = compliment_vassal
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAheir13

		event_target:compliment_vassal = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_impressed who = event_target:introduced_heir years = 20 }
		}
	}
	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}
}

# heir.15 A new friend
# The heir befriends a vassal's child.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = heir.15
	desc = EVTDESCheir15
	picture = GFX_evt_child_sword

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes }
		any_vassal = {
			ai = yes
			prisoner = no
			NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			any_child = {
				ai = yes
				age = 8
				is_adult = no
				prisoner = no
				is_landed = no
				NOR = {
					is_friend = event_target:introduced_heir
					is_rival = event_target:introduced_heir
					sibling = event_target:introduced_heir
					trait = incapable
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes } } }
			clr_flags_introduce_heir_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		any_vassal = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				prisoner = no
				NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			any_child = {
				limit = {
					ai = yes
					age = 8
					is_adult = no
					prisoner = no
					is_landed = no
					NOR = {
						is_friend = event_target:introduced_heir
						is_rival = event_target:introduced_heir
						sibling = event_target:introduced_heir
						trait = incapable
						is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = befriended_child
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAheir15

		event_target:introduced_heir = {
			show_scope_change = no
			add_friend = event_target:befriended_child
		}
	}
	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}
}

# heir.16 A new rival
# The heir fights a vassal's child.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = heir.16
	desc = EVTDESCheir16
	picture = GFX_evt_child_bully

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes }
		any_vassal = {
			ai = yes
			prisoner = no
			NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			any_child = {
				ai = yes
				age = 8
				is_adult = no
				prisoner = no
				is_landed = no
				NOR = {
					is_friend = event_target:introduced_heir
					is_rival = event_target:introduced_heir
					sibling = event_target:introduced_heir
					trait = incapable
					is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes } } }
			clr_flags_introduce_heir_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		any_vassal = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				prisoner = no
				NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			any_child = {
				limit = {
					ai = yes
					age = 8
					is_adult = no
					prisoner = no
					is_landed = no

						is_friend = event_target:introduced_heir
						is_rival = event_target:introduced_heir
						sibling = event_target:introduced_heir

						is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
					}

		}

		hidden_effect = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}
}

# heir.17 The heir's spirits sink
# The formalities wear the heir down.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = heir.17
	desc = EVTDESCheir17
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes }
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes } } }
			clr_flags_introduce_heir_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAheir17

		event_target:introduced_heir = {
			add_trait = depressed
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = 38314 days = 1000 random = 800 } }
		}
	}
	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}
}

# heir.18 A warrior offers lessons
# A vassal known for war offers to teach the heir to fight.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = heir.18
	desc = EVTDESCheir18
	picture = GFX_evt_family_feud_republic

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes }
		any_vassal = {
			ai = yes
			prisoner = no
			NOR = { holds_favor_on = ROOT trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			OR = { trait = strategist trait = duelist martial = 12 }
		}
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes } } }
			clr_flags_introduce_heir_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		any_vassal = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				prisoner = no
				NOR = { holds_favor_on = ROOT trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				OR = { trait = strategist trait = duelist martial = 12 }
			}
			save_event_target_as = vassal_teacher_martial
		}
	}

	#Accept offer
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAheir18

		event_target:introduced_heir = { change_martial = 1 prestige = 50 }
		event_target:vassal_teacher_martial = { add_favor = ROOT }
	}

	#Decline offer
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTBheir18

		prestige = -50

		event_target:vassal_teacher_martial = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_disappointed who = ROOT years = 10 }
		}
	}
	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}
}

# heir.19 Puppy love
# The heir follows a vassal's wife around, and the husband is not amused.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = heir.19
	desc = EVTDESCheir19
	picture = GFX_evt_princess_byzantine

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes }
		any_vassal = {
			is_female = no
			ai = yes
			is_married = yes
			prisoner = no
			NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			any_spouse = {
				is_female = yes
				ai = yes
				prisoner = no
				age = 20
				is_landed = no
				NOT = { age = 45 }
				NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:introduced_heir = { is_alive = yes } } }
			clr_flags_introduce_heir_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		random_vassal = {
			limit = {
				is_female = no
				ai = yes
				is_married = yes
				prisoner = no
				NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				any_spouse = {
					is_female = yes
					ai = yes
					prisoner = no
					age = 20
					is_landed = no
					NOT = { age = 45 }
					NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = husband_target
			random_spouse = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
					ai = yes
					prisoner = no
					age = 20
					is_landed = no
					NOT = { age = 45 }
					NOR = { trait = incapable is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				}
				save_event_target_as = wife_target
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAheir19

		event_target:introduced_heir = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = teenage_infatuation who = event_target:wife_target years = 10 }
		}

		event_target:husband_target = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = {
				modifier = opinion_disapprove
				who = event_target:introduced_heir
				years = 10
			}
		}
	}
	after = {
		hidden_effect = {
			character_event = { id = heir.1 days = 10 random = 5 } #send tombola again
		}
	}
}
